def validate(self) -> "SimConfig":         def need(cond: bool, msg: str):             if not cond:                 raise ValueError(f"invalid SimConfig: {msg}")         c, p, d, t, r, o, m = (self.compute, self.power, self.dvfs, self.thermal,                                self.radiation, self.orbital, self.memory)         need(c.array_size > 0, "array_size must be > 0")         need(c.area_mm2 > 0, "area_mm2 must be > 0")         need(0.0 <= p.static_frac < 1.0, "static_frac must be in [0,1)")         need(p.clock_frac + p.interconnect_frac < 1.0, "clock_frac + interconnect_frac must be < 1")         need(p.base_power_w > 0, "base_power_w must be > 0")         need(p.T_nom_k < p.T_max_k, "T_nom_k < T_max_k")         need(d.nom_freq_ghz > 0, "nom_freq_ghz must be > 0")         need(c.process_node.min_voltage() <= d.nom_voltage_v <= c.process_node.max_voltage(),              f"nom_voltage_v outside {c.process_node.value} range")         need(d.nom_freq_ghz <= c.process_node.max_freq_ghz(), "nom_freq_ghz exceeds node maximum")         need(0.0 < d.throttle_floor <= 1.0, "throttle_floor must be in (0,1]")         need(t.capacitance_j_per_k > 0 and t.radiator_area_m2 > 0, "thermal capacitance/area must be > 0")         need(0.0 < t.emissivity <= 1.0 and 0.0 < t.view_factor <= 1.0, "emissivity/view_factor in (0,1]")         need(m.onchip_sram_mb > 0 and m.hbm_bw_tb_s_per_stack > 0 and m.num_hbm_stacks > 0, "memory sizes > 0")         need(0.0 <= r.mbu_prob <= 1.0 and r.mbu_max_cluster >= 2, "mbu params")         need(r.ecc_interleave >= 1 and r.ecc_word_bits >= 1, "ecc geometry")         need(r.scrub_interval_s > 0, "scrub_interval_s must be > 0")         for name in ("avf_sram", "avf_register", "avf_logic", "secded_odd_miscorrect_prob"):             need(0.0 <= getattr(r, name) <= 1.0, f"{name} in [0,1]")         need(0.0 <= o.inclination_deg <= 180.0, "inclination_deg in [0,180]")         if o.eclipse_fraction_override is not None:             need(0.0 <= o.eclipse_fraction_override < 1.0, "eclipse_fraction_override in [0,1)")         need(c.sparse_speedup_at_50 >= 1.0, "sparse_speedup_at_50 >= 1")         need(0.0 <= c.pipeline_overlap <= 1.0, "pipeline_overlap in [0,1]")         need(0.0 < m.hbm_efficiency <= 1.0, "hbm_efficiency in (0,1]")         need(0.0 < r.mbu_cluster_geom_p <= 1.0, "mbu_cluster_geom_p in (0,1]")         need(r.environment_mode in ("scaled_placeholder", "tabulated_orbit"), "unknown environment_mode")         need(o.sep_mean_duration_s > 0 and o.sep_intensity_median > 0 and o.sep_intensity_log_sigma >= 0,              "SEP statistics must be positive")         need(o.sep_events_per_year is None or o.sep_events_per_year >= 0, "sep_events_per_year >= 0")         for key, tab in r.tables.items():             need(key in _SPECTRUM_KEYS, f"unknown spectrum table '{key}'")             xs_, ph_ = np.asarray(tab.x, float), np.asarray(tab.phi_integral, float)             need(len(xs_) >= 2 and len(xs_) == len(ph_), f"table '{key}' needs >= 2 matched points")             need(bool(np.all(np.diff(xs_) > 0) and np.all(xs_ > 0)), f"table '{key}': x must be positive ascending")             need(bool(np.all(ph_ >= 0) and np.all(np.diff(ph_) <= 0)), f"table '{key}': flux must be >= 0, non-increasing")         need(r.environment_mode != "tabulated_orbit" or len(r.tables) > 0, "tabulated_orbit needs at least one table")         # cross-section grids must start below every threshold, or flux would be silently dropped         for reg in (r.sram, r.register, r.logic_set, r.sel):             need(reg.ion.x_th > r.let_grid_lo, "ion x_th must exceed let_grid_lo")             need(reg.proton.x_th > r.energy_grid_lo, "proton x_th must exceed energy_grid_lo")         return self     # ------------------------------------------------------------------     def copy(self) -> "SimConfig":         return copy.deepcopy(self)     def to_dict(self) -> dict:         return json.loads(json.dumps(asdict(self), default=str))     def to_json(self, indent: int = 2) -> str:         return json.dumps(asdict(self), indent=indent, default=str, sort_keys=True)     def config_hash(self) -> str:         return hashlib.sha256(self.to_json(indent=0).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]     # ---- round trip -------------------------------------------------------     @classmethod     def from_dict(cls, d: dict) -> "SimConfig":         return _dc_from_dict(cls, d)     @classmethod     def from_json(cls, text: str) -> "SimConfig":         return cls.from_dict(json.loads(text)) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # Generic dict -> dataclass reconstruction (enums, nested dataclasses, Optional, Dict, List) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- def _build(tp, val):     if val is None:         return None     origin, args = typing.get_origin(tp), typing.get_args(tp)     if dataclasses.is_dataclass(tp):         return _dc_from_dict(tp, val)     if isinstance(tp, type) and issubclass(tp, Enum):         return tp[val.split(".")[-1]] if isinstance(val, str) else tp(val)     if origin is typing.Union:         inner = [a for a in args if a is not type(None)]         return _build(inner[0], val)     if origin in (dict, Dict):         return {k: _build(args[1], v) for k, v in val.items()}     if origin in (list, List):         return [_build(args[0], v) for v in val]     if tp is float:         return float(val)     if tp is int:         return int(val)     return val def _dc_from_dict(cls, d: dict):     hints = typing.get_type_hints(cls)     names = {f.name for f in dataclasses.fields(cls)}     unknown = set(d) - names     if unknown:         raise ValueError(f"{cls.__name__}: unknown field(s) {sorted(unknown)}")     return cls(**{k: _build(hints[k], v) for k, v in d.items()}) ``` ### `uchu/memory.py` ```python """Roofline / performance model. v3 fixes vs v2   * arithmetic intensity & traffic use the real GEMM dims (M, K, N), not M alone   * HBM traffic from an explicit blocking model (operand re-reads when SRAM is too small)   * the layer's own dtype is used (v2 used a global dtype)   * sparsity is applied ONCE (executed work shrinks; peak throughput is unchanged)   * array tiling quantisation and pipeline fill/drain are modelled via an exact cycle count     (fill/drain fully exposed per tile unless ComputeConfig.pipeline_overlap > 0: conservative)   * the inverted dtype throughput table is corrected (see config.DType) """ from __future__ import annotations import math from dataclasses import dataclass from typing import List from .config import DType, SimConfig @dataclass(frozen=True) class WorkloadLayer:     name: str     kind: str     M: int     K: int     N: int     dtype: DType = DType.INT8     sparsity: float = 0.0   # 0.0 (dense) or 0.5 (2:4 structured) -- the only patterns hardware supports     def __post_init__(self):         if min(self.M, self.K, self.N) <= 0:             raise ValueError(f"layer {self.name}: M, K, N must be positive")         if self.sparsity not in (0.0, 0.5):             raise ValueError(f"layer {self.name}: only 0.0 or 0.5 (2:4) sparsity is supported, "                              f"got {self.sparsity}")     @property     def dense_flops(self) -> float:         return 2.0 * self.M * self.K * self.N def structured_sparsity_speedup(sparsity: float, speedup_at_50: float = 1.85) -> float:     """Compute-bound speedup of 2:4 structured sparsity.  All-or-nothing: 0.0 -> 1, 0.5 -> speedup_at_50."""     if sparsity == 0.0:         return 1.0     if sparsity == 0.5:         return float(speedup_at_50)     raise ValueError("2:4 hardware supports only sparsity 0.0 or 0.5") def weight_bytes_factor(sparsity: float, bytes_per_elem: float) -> float:     """Stored weight bytes relative to dense: kept values + 2-bit index per kept value."""     if sparsity == 0.0:         return 1.0     kept = 1.0 - sparsity     return kept * (1.0 + 2.0 / (8.0 * bytes_per_elem)) @dataclass(frozen=True) class LayerPerf:     name: str     duration_s: float     t_compute_s: float     t_memory_s: float     memory_bound: bool     dense_flops: float     executed_flops: float     hbm_bytes: float     arithmetic_intensity: float      # executed flops / HBM byte     ridge_point: float               # flops/byte at which compute == memory roofs     util: float                      # fraction of peak MAC slots doing useful work, in [0,1]     resident_sram_bits: float     peak_exec_tflops: float def hbm_bandwidth_bytes_s(cfg: SimConfig) -> float:     m = cfg.memory     eff = 1.0 / (1.0 + m.ecc_bw_overhead_pct / 100.0) if m.ecc_enabled else 1.0     return m.hbm_bw_tb_s_per_stack * 1e12 * m.num_hbm_stacks * eff * m.hbm_efficiency def estimate_layer(layer: WorkloadLayer, cfg: SimConfig, freq_ghz: float, exact_cycles: bool = True) -> LayerPerf:     """``exact_cycles=False`` drops the ceil() on the reduction depth (< 1 cycle per tile): used only by the calibration fits,     whose objective must be smooth in ``sparse_speedup_at_50``."""     A = cfg.compute.array_size     b = layer.dtype.bytes_per_elem     thr = layer.dtype.pe_throughput     f_hz = freq_ghz * 1e9     M, K, N = layer.M, layer.K, layer.N     spd = structured_sparsity_speedup(layer.sparsity, cfg.compute.sparse_speedup_at_50)     K_eff = K / spd                                   # executed reduction depth     # --- compute: output-stationary AxA array, exact cycle count -----------------     tiles = math.ceil(M / A) * math.ceil(N / A)     fill = 2 * A - 2                                        # systolic fill + drain skew     k_cycles = math.ceil(K_eff / thr) if exact_cycles else K_eff / thr     cycles = (tiles * k_cycles               + fill * (1.0 + (tiles - 1) * (1.0 - cfg.compute.pipeline_overlap)))     t_compute = cycles / f_hz     ideal_cycles = M * N * K_eff / (A * A * thr)     # --- memory: blocked GEMM traffic ---------------------------------------------     sram_bytes = cfg.memory.onchip_sram_mb * 2**20     T = max(1, int(math.sqrt(sram_bytes / (3.0 * b))))      # square tile edge, 3 operand tiles resident     wfac = weight_bytes_factor(layer.sparsity, b)     hbm_bytes = b * (M * K * math.ceil(N / T)                      + wfac * K * N * math.ceil(M / T)                      + M * N)     bw = hbm_bandwidth_bytes_s(cfg)     t_mem = hbm_bytes / bw     duration = max(t_compute, t_mem)     executed = 2.0 * M * N * K_eff     peak_exec = 2.0 * A * A * f_hz * thr     util = min(1.0, ideal_cycles / (duration * f_hz))     working_set_bits = 8.0 * b * (M * K + wfac * K * N + M * N)     resident = min(sram_bytes * 8.0, working_set_bits)     return LayerPerf(         name=layer.name, duration_s=duration, t_compute_s=t_compute, t_memory_s=t_mem,         memory_bound=t_mem > t_compute, dense_flops=layer.dense_flops, executed_flops=executed,         hbm_bytes=hbm_bytes, arithmetic_intensity=executed / hbm_bytes,         ridge_point=peak_exec / bw, util=util, resident_sram_bits=resident,         peak_exec_tflops=peak_exec / 1e12) @dataclass(frozen=True) class PassProfile:     """Time-weighted summary of one forward pass at a fixed clock."""     duration_s: float     dense_flops: float     executed_flops: float     util: float     live_sram_bits: float     memory_bound_fraction: float     layers: tuple def profile_pass(layers: List[WorkloadLayer], cfg: SimConfig, freq_ghz: float) -> PassProfile:     perfs = [estimate_layer(l, cfg, freq_ghz) for l in layers]     dur = sum(p.duration_s for p in perfs)     return PassProfile(         duration_s=dur,         dense_flops=sum(p.dense_flops for p in perfs),         executed_flops=sum(p.executed_flops for p in perfs),         util=sum(p.util * p.duration_s for p in perfs) / dur,         live_sram_bits=sum(p.resident_sram_bits * p.duration_s for p in perfs) / dur,         memory_bound_fraction=sum(p.duration_s for p in perfs if p.memory_bound) / dur,         layers=tuple(perfs)) def build_transformer_workload(seq_len: int = 512, hidden_dim: int = 1024, num_heads: int = 16,                                num_layers: int = 12, ffn_mult: int = 4, batch_size: int = 1,                                dtype: DType = DType.INT8, sparsity: float = 0.0) -> List[WorkloadLayer]:     d, ff, B, S = hidden_dim, hidden_dim * ffn_mult, batch_size, seq_len     hd = d // num_heads     out: List[WorkloadLayer] = []     for i in range(num_layers):         p = f"L{i:02d}"         out += [             WorkloadLayer(f"{p}/QKV", "linear", B * S, d, 3 * d, dtype, sparsity),             WorkloadLayer(f"{p}/AttnScore", "attention", B * num_heads * S, hd, S, dtype, 0.0),             WorkloadLayer(f"{p}/AttnOut", "attention", B * num_heads * S, S, hd, dtype, 0.0),             WorkloadLayer(f"{p}/OutProj", "linear", B * S, d, d, dtype, sparsity),             WorkloadLayer(f"{p}/FFN1", "linear", B * S, d, ff, dtype, sparsity),             WorkloadLayer(f"{p}/FFN2", "linear", B * S, ff, d, dtype, sparsity),         ]     return out ``` ### `uchu/orbit.py` ```python """Orbital environment: geometry (analytic eclipse), SEP-event schedule. v3 design: the environment is *stateless in time* except for a lazily generated flare schedule.  The simulator owns the single clock and queries exact interval quantities (eclipse segments, integrated flare intensity), so there is no per-layer ``advance_time`` drift. """ from __future__ import annotations import math from typing import List, Optional, Sequence, Tuple import numpy as np from .config import OrbitalConfig, OrbitType, SolarActivity MU_EARTH = 3.986004418e14   # m^3 / s^2 R_EARTH_KM = 6378.137 # SEP-event (solar energetic particle) arrival rates, events / year   -- PLACEHOLDER SEP_EVENTS_PER_YEAR = {SolarActivity.LOW: 0.5, SolarActivity.MODERATE: 3.0, SolarActivity.HIGH: 8.0} SEP_MEAN_DURATION_S = 12 * 3600.0          # PLACEHOLDER SEP_INTENSITY_LOGSIGMA = 1.0               # lognormal sigma, median intensity 1.0 (PLACEHOLDER) def kepler_period_s(altitude_km: float) -> float:     a = (R_EARTH_KM + altitude_km) * 1e3     return 2.0 * math.pi * math.sqrt(a ** 3 / MU_EARTH) def eclipse_fraction(altitude_km: float, beta_deg: float = 0.0) -> float:     """Fraction of a circular orbit spent in Earth's cylindrical shadow."""     R, h = R_EARTH_KM, altitude_km     beta = math.radians(beta_deg)     arg = math.sqrt(h * h + 2.0 * R * h) / ((R + h) * math.cos(beta))     if arg >= 1.0:         return 0.0     return math.acos(arg) / math.pi class FlareSchedule:     """Poisson process of SEP events; each has (start, end, intensity).  Generated lazily and     sequentially from one RNG, so the schedule prefix is independent of query order."""     def __init__(self, rate_per_s: float, rng: Optional[np.random.Generator],                  mean_duration_s: float = SEP_MEAN_DURATION_S,                  log_sigma: float = SEP_INTENSITY_LOGSIGMA, median: float = 1.0):         self.rate = rate_per_s         self.median = median         self.rng = rng         self.mean_dur = mean_duration_s         self.log_sigma = log_sigma         self._cursor = 0.0         self._ev: List[Tuple[float, float, float]] = []         self._arr = (np.empty(0), np.empty(0), np.empty(0))         self._fixed = False     @classmethod     def from_events(cls, events: Sequence[Tuple[float, float, float]]) -> "FlareSchedule":         """Deterministic schedule for tests / replaying a real event list: (start_s, end_s, intensity)."""         s = cls(0.0, None)         s._ev = [tuple(map(float, e)) for e in events]         s._fixed = True         s._rebuild()         return s     def _rebuild(self):         if self._ev:             a = np.array(self._ev, float)             self._arr = (a[:, 0], a[:, 1], a[:, 2])         else:             self._arr = (np.empty(0), np.empty(0), np.empty(0))     def extend_to(self, t: float) -> None:         if self._fixed or self.rate <= 0.0:             return         grew = False         while self._cursor <= t:             self._cursor += float(self.rng.exponential(1.0 / self.rate))             dur = float(self.rng.exponential(self.mean_dur))             inten = float(self.rng.lognormal(math.log(self.median), self.log_sigma))             self._ev.append((self._cursor, self._cursor + dur, inten))             grew = True         if grew:             self._rebuild()     def intensity_integral(self, t0: float, t1: float) -> float:         """Exact integral of summed flare intensity over [t0, t1]  [intensity * s]."""         if t1 <= t0:             return 0.0         self.extend_to(t1)         s, e, i = self._arr         if s.size == 0:             return 0.0         overlap = np.clip(np.minimum(e, t1) - np.maximum(s, t0), 0.0, None)         return float(np.sum(i * overlap))     def intensity_at(self, t: float) -> float:         self.extend_to(t)         s, e, i = self._arr         if s.size == 0:             return 0.0         return float(np.sum(i[(s <= t) & (t < e)]))     @property     def events(self) -> List[Tuple[float, float, float]]:         return list(self._ev) class OrbitalEnvironment:     def __init__(self, cfg: OrbitalConfig, rng: Optional[np.random.Generator] = None):         self.cfg = cfg         if cfg.orbit_type == OrbitType.DEEP:             self.period_s = 1e12             self.eclipse_fraction = 0.0         else:             self.period_s = kepler_period_s(cfg.altitude_km)             self.eclipse_fraction = (cfg.eclipse_fraction_override                                      if cfg.eclipse_fraction_override is not None                                      else eclipse_fraction(cfg.altitude_km, cfg.beta_deg))         per_year = (cfg.sep_events_per_year if cfg.sep_events_per_year is not None                     else SEP_EVENTS_PER_YEAR[cfg.solar_activity])         self.flares = FlareSchedule(per_year / (365.25 * 86400.0),                                     rng if rng is not None else np.random.default_rng(0),                                     mean_duration_s=cfg.sep_mean_duration_s,                                     log_sigma=cfg.sep_intensity_log_sigma, median=cfg.sep_intensity_median)     # ---- geometry ---------------------------------------------------------     def in_eclipse(self, t: float) -> bool:         if self.eclipse_fraction <= 0.0:             return False         return (t % self.period_s) < self.eclipse_fraction * self.period_s     def eclipse_segments(self, t0: float, t1: float) -> List[Tuple[float, float, bool]]:         """Partition [t0, t1] into maximal (a, b, in_eclipse) segments, exactly."""         if t1 <= t0:             return []         if self.eclipse_fraction <= 0.0:             return [(t0, t1, False)]         P = self.period_s         ecl = self.eclipse_fraction * P         segs: List[Tuple[float, float, bool]] = []         t = t0         k = int(math.floor(t0 / P))         while t < t1:             start = k * P             ecl_end = start + ecl             nxt = start + P             if t < ecl_end:                 b = min(ecl_end, t1)                 segs.append((t, b, True))                 t = b             if t < t1:                 b = min(nxt, t1)                 if b > t:                     segs.append((t, b, False))                     t = b             k += 1         return segs     # ---- SEP events ---------------------------------------------------------     def flare_intensity_integral(self, t0: float, t1: float) -> float:         return self.flares.intensity_integral(t0, t1)     def flare_intensity_at(self, t: float) -> float:         return self.flares.intensity_at(t) ``` ### `uchu/thermal.py` ```python """Power, DVFS and the single lumped thermal model. v3 fixes vs v2   * ONE thermal model.  v2 integrated an ODE but then overwrote the state every     layer with a static ``250 + 0.8 P`` estimate.   * implicit (backward-Euler) integration with the leakage feedback solved     inside the step: unconditionally stable (v2 used forward Euler with C = 0.5 J/K).   * node leakage factor scales only STATIC power, area scales static power, MAC count scales     dynamic power (v2 multiplied total power by area x array^2 x leakage).   * clock/interconnect power also scale with V^2 (v2 omitted V).   * Earth IR + albedo loads added; radiator sized so default dissipation is feasible.   * runaway is detected and reported instead of silently clamped. """ from __future__ import annotations import math from dataclasses import dataclass from typing import Callable, Optional, Tuple from .config import ProcessNode, SimConfig from .orbit import OrbitType SIGMA_SB = 5.670374419e-8 T_MIN_K = 3.0 T_SEARCH_MAX_K = 2500.0 @dataclass(frozen=True) class DVFSState:     freq_ghz: float     voltage_v: float     throttled: bool class PowerModel:     """P = P_leak(T, V) + P_clock(f, V) + util * [P_logic + P_interconnect](f, V)."""     def __init__(self, cfg: SimConfig):         self.cfg = cfg         pw, comp = cfg.power, cfg.compute         self.pw, self.dv, self.node = pw, cfg.dvfs, comp.process_node         ref = ProcessNode[pw.ref_node]         area_ratio = comp.area_mm2 / pw.ref_area_mm2         self.array_ratio = (comp.array_size / pw.ref_array_size) ** 2         leak_rel = self.node.leakage_factor() / ref.leakage_factor()         cap_rel = self.node.cap_factor() / ref.cap_factor()         self.static_nom_w = pw.base_power_w * pw.static_frac * area_ratio * leak_rel         self.dynamic_nom_w = pw.base_power_w * (1.0 - pw.static_frac) * self.array_ratio * cap_rel         self.ic_scale = self.array_ratio ** pw.interconnect_extra_exponent     def breakdown(self, T: float, f_ghz: float, v: float, util: float = 1.0) -> dict:         pw = self.pw         fr = f_ghz / self.dv.nom_freq_ghz         vr = v / self.dv.nom_voltage_v         dyn = self.dynamic_nom_w * fr * vr ** 2         return {             "leakage": self.static_nom_w * math.exp(pw.leak_temp_coeff * (T - pw.T_nom_k) / pw.T_nom_k) * vr ** 3,             "clock": dyn * pw.clock_frac,             "interconnect": dyn * pw.interconnect_frac * self.ic_scale * util,             "logic": dyn * (1.0 - pw.clock_frac - pw.interconnect_frac) * util,         }     def power_w(self, T: float, f_ghz: float, v: float, util: float = 1.0) -> float:         return sum(self.breakdown(T, f_ghz, v, util).values())     def dvfs_state(self, T: float, f_target_ghz: Optional[float] = None) -> DVFSState:         dv = self.dv         f_t = dv.nom_freq_ghz if f_target_ghz is None else f_target_ghz         excess = max(0.0, T - dv.throttle_onset_k)         f = f_t * max(dv.throttle_floor, 1.0 - dv.throttle_slope_per_k * excess)         f = min(f, self.node.max_freq_ghz())         if dv.voltage_scaling:             v = dv.nom_voltage_v * math.sqrt(f / dv.nom_freq_ghz)             v = min(max(v, self.node.min_voltage()), self.node.max_voltage())         else:             v = dv.nom_voltage_v         return DVFSState(f, v, throttled=f < 0.99 * f_t) def _bracket(g: Callable[[float], float], T0: float, g0: float, direction: int              ) -> Optional[Tuple[float, float]]:     """Find [a, b] bracketing the nearest sign change of g from T0 in `direction` (doubling steps)."""     step = 0.05     a, ga = T0, g0     while True:         b = min(max(T0 + direction * step, T_MIN_K), T_SEARCH_MAX_K)         gb = g(b)         if ga == 0.0:             return (a, a)         if ga * gb <= 0.0:             return (a, b)         if b in (T_MIN_K, T_SEARCH_MAX_K):             return None         a, ga = b, gb         step *= 2.0 class LumpedThermalModel:     def __init__(self, cfg: SimConfig):         self.tc = cfg.thermal         self.orbit_type = cfg.orbital.orbit_type         self.T = cfg.thermal.T_init_k         self.runaway = False     # ---- loads -----------------------------------------------------------     @property     def effective_view_factor(self) -> float:         tc = self.tc         return max(0.1, tc.view_factor * (1.0 - tc.degradation_per_year * tc.mission_year))     def radiated_w(self, T: float) -> float:         tc = self.tc         return tc.emissivity * SIGMA_SB * self.effective_view_factor * tc.radiator_area_m2 * T ** 4     def env_load_w(self, eclipse: bool) -> float:         tc = self.tc         A = tc.radiator_area_m2         if self.orbit_type == OrbitType.DEEP:             return tc.absorptance * tc.solar_flux_w_m2 * tc.sun_projected_fraction * A         ir = tc.emissivity * tc.earth_ir_w_m2 * tc.earth_view_fraction * A         if eclipse:             return ir         solar = tc.absorptance * tc.solar_flux_w_m2 * tc.sun_projected_fraction * A         alb = (tc.absorptance * tc.albedo * tc.solar_flux_w_m2 * tc.earth_view_fraction                * tc.albedo_geometry * A)         return ir + solar + alb     # ---- integration -------------------------------------------------------     def step(self, dt: float, power_fn: Callable[[float], float], eclipse: bool) -> float:         """Backward-Euler step of  C dT/dt = P(T) + Q_env - P_rad(T).  Returns the new temperature."""         if dt <= 0.0:             return self.T         C, T0, Q = self.tc.capacitance_j_per_k, self.T, self.env_load_w(eclipse)         def g(T: float) -> float:             return C * (T - T0) / dt - power_fn(T) - Q + self.radiated_w(T)         g0 = g(T0)         if g0 == 0.0:             return T0         br = _bracket(g, T0, g0, +1 if g0 < 0.0 else -1)         if br is None:             if g0 < 0.0:                 # heating with no root below the search ceiling: runaway                 self.runaway = True                 self.T = T_SEARCH_MAX_K             else:                 self.T = T_MIN_K             return self.T         from scipy.optimize import brentq         a, b = br         self.T = a if a == b else brentq(g, min(a, b), max(a, b), xtol=1e-10, rtol=1e-13, maxiter=200)         return self.T     def steady_state(self, power_fn: Callable[[float], float], eclipse: bool) -> Optional[float]:         """Lowest (stable) equilibrium of P(T) + Q = P_rad(T); None if no equilibrium (runaway)."""         import numpy as np         from scipy.optimize import brentq         Q = self.env_load_w(eclipse)         def h(T: float) -> float:             return self.radiated_w(T) - power_fn(T) - Q         grid = np.geomspace(T_MIN_K, T_SEARCH_MAX_K, 800)         vals = [h(float(T)) for T in grid]         for i in range(len(grid) - 1):             if vals[i] <= 0.0 < vals[i + 1]:                 return float(brentq(h, grid[i], grid[i + 1], xtol=1e-10))         return None ``` ### `uchu/radiation.py` ```python """Radiation core (RHBD v4): cross-section x flux physics, word-level ECC, accumulation, scrubbing. Replaces the v2 model, in which   * rates were a single made-up per-bit constant times ad-hoc orbit multipliers,   * errors were injected into a 64-element array of zeros (rates independent of the workload),   * "SDC" was just the uncorrectable count,   * scrubbing declared every upset corrected (no accumulation => no double-bit errors),   * flare intensity was sampled but never used, and GCR rose with solar activity (it falls). Physics implemented here   rate/unit = integral sigma(x) * (-dPhi/dx) dx      (x = LET for ions, E for protons)   four environment components: GCR ions, trapped protons, SEP ions, SEP protons   SRAM: Poisson upsets -> MBU clusters on adjacent physical bits -> bit-interleaved SECDED words         -> persistent flipped-bit state until the next scrub -> {corrected, DUE, SDC-candidate}   registers / logic (SET): derated by capture probability, AVF and optional TMR   SEL: Poisson events -> power-cycle downtime (protected) or destruction (unprotected) """ from __future__ import annotations import math from dataclasses import dataclass, field from typing import Dict, Optional import numpy as np from .config import RadiationConfig, SimConfig, SolarActivity from .config import OrbitType from .orbit import OrbitalEnvironment from .stats import poisson_rate_ci # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # Spectra and the cross-section integral # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- @dataclass(frozen=True) class TruncatedPowerLaw:     """Integral flux Phi(>x) = phi_ref [ (x/x_ref)^-g - (x_max/x_ref)^-g ]  for x < x_max, else 0."""     phi_ref: float     x_ref: float     gamma: float     x_max: float     def integral(self, x):         x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)         v = self.phi_ref * (np.power(np.maximum(x, 1e-300) / self.x_ref, -self.gamma)                             - (self.x_max / self.x_ref) ** (-self.gamma))         return np.where(x < self.x_max, v, 0.0)     def differential(self, x):         x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)         d = self.gamma * self.phi_ref / self.x_ref * np.power(np.maximum(x, 1e-300) / self.x_ref,                                                                  -self.gamma - 1.0)         return np.where(x < self.x_max, d, 0.0) class TabulatedSpectrum:     """Integral flux from a CREME96/OMERE/SPENVIS export: arrays x (ascending) and Phi(>x)."""     def __init__(self, x, phi_integral):         self.x = np.asarray(x, float)         self.phi = np.asarray(phi_integral, float)         if np.any(np.diff(self.x) <= 0) or np.any(np.diff(self.phi) > 0):             raise ValueError("x must be ascending and Phi(>x) non-increasing")         self.x_max = float(self.x[-1])     def integral(self, x):         x = np.asarray(x, float)         lx, lp = np.log(self.x), np.log(np.maximum(self.phi, 1e-300))         out = np.exp(np.interp(np.log(np.maximum(x, 1e-300)), lx, lp))         out = np.where(x < self.x[0], self.phi[0], out)         return np.where(x < self.x_max, out, 0.0) def integral_rate(sigma, spectrum, x_lo: float, x_hi: Optional[float] = None,                   n: int = 4000, extra_nodes=()) -> float:     """sum_bins sigma(x_mid) * [Phi(x_i) - Phi(x_{i+1})]   (units of sigma x flux: events / unit / s).     Flux mass per bin is exact (differences of the integral flux); only sigma is sampled.     Adding ``extra_nodes`` (e.g. a threshold) makes step cross-sections exact.     """     x_hi = float(spectrum.x_max if x_hi is None else x_hi)     grid = np.geomspace(x_lo, x_hi, n + 1)     if len(extra_nodes):         ex = np.asarray([e for e in extra_nodes if x_lo < e < x_hi], float)         grid = np.unique(np.concatenate([grid, ex]))     dphi = spectrum.integral(grid[:-1]) - spectrum.integral(grid[1:])     mid = np.sqrt(grid[:-1] * grid[1:])     return float(np.sum(sigma(mid) * dphi)) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # Orbit / shielding scalings (PLACEHOLDERS -- replace with CREME96 / AP-9 derived tables) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- _SOLAR_GCR_MOD = {SolarActivity.LOW: 1.0, SolarActivity.MODERATE: 0.7, SolarActivity.HIGH: 0.45} _TRAPPED_ALT_KM = np.array([200., 400., 600., 800., 1000., 1500., 2000.]) _TRAPPED_FACTOR = np.array([0.05, 0.4, 1.0, 3.0, 8.0, 40.0, 60.0])   # 1.0 == reference spectrum def geomagnetic_transmission(orbit_type: OrbitType, inclination_deg: float) -> float:     if orbit_type == OrbitType.LEO:         return 0.05 + 0.95 * abs(math.sin(math.radians(inclination_deg))) ** 3     if orbit_type == OrbitType.MEO:         return 0.9     return 1.0 def trapped_proton_factor(orbit_type: OrbitType, altitude_km: float) -> float:     if orbit_type == OrbitType.LEO:         return float(np.exp(np.interp(altitude_km, _TRAPPED_ALT_KM, np.log(_TRAPPED_FACTOR))))     if orbit_type == OrbitType.MEO:         return 300.0     return 0.0          # GEO / deep space: no significant trapped-proton population class RadiationEnvironment:     """Time-dependent expected upset counts for each region, from cross-section x flux integrals."""     REGIONS = ("sram", "register", "logic_set", "sel")     def __init__(self, cfg: SimConfig, orbit: OrbitalEnvironment):         rc: RadiationConfig = cfg.radiation         self.orbit = orbit         o = cfg.orbital         tabulated = rc.environment_mode == "tabulated_orbit"         if tabulated:        # orbit-specific, already-shielded tables: no placeholder scalings             geo = solar = trapped = p_shield = 1.0         else:             geo = geomagnetic_transmission(o.orbit_type, o.inclination_deg)             solar = _SOLAR_GCR_MOD[o.solar_activity]             trapped = trapped_proton_factor(o.orbit_type, o.altitude_km)             p_shield = math.exp(-rc.shield_mm_al / rc.shield_atten_len_mm)         self.scalars = {"geo": geo, "solar_gcr": solar, "trapped": trapped, "proton_shield": p_shield}         spec = {}         for k in ("gcr_ion", "trapped_proton", "sep_ion", "sep_proton"):             if k in rc.tables:                 spec[k] = TabulatedSpectrum(rc.tables[k].x, rc.tables[k].phi_integral)             elif tabulated:                 spec[k] = None                      # component absent from the orbit-specific tables => zero flux             else:                 spec[k] = TruncatedPowerLaw(**vars(getattr(rc, k)))         def _rate(cs, key, lo):             return 0.0 if spec[key] is None else integral_rate(cs, spec[key], lo, extra_nodes=[cs.x_th])         self.base: Dict[str, Dict[str, float]] = {}         self.static_rate: Dict[str, float] = {}     # events / unit / s outside SEP events         self.sep_coeff: Dict[str, float] = {}       # events / unit / (intensity * s)         for name in self.REGIONS:             xs = getattr(rc, name)             comp = {                 "gcr": _rate(xs.ion, "gcr_ion", rc.let_grid_lo),                 "trapped": _rate(xs.proton, "trapped_proton", rc.energy_grid_lo),                 "sep_ion": _rate(xs.ion, "sep_ion", rc.let_grid_lo),                 "sep_p": _rate(xs.proton, "sep_proton", rc.energy_grid_lo),             }             self.base[name] = comp             self.static_rate[name] = comp["gcr"] * geo * solar + comp["trapped"] * trapped * p_shield             self.sep_coeff[name] = (comp["sep_ion"] + comp["sep_p"] * p_shield) * geo     def rate_per_unit_s(self, region: str, t: float) -> float:         return self.static_rate[region] + self.sep_coeff[region] * self.orbit.flare_intensity_at(t)     def expected(self, region: str, n_units: float, t0: float, t1: float) -> float:         """Expected event count for n_units (bits / gates / devices) over [t0, t1], exact in the flare term."""         if t1 <= t0 or n_units <= 0:             return 0.0         return float(n_units) * (self.static_rate[region] * (t1 - t0)                                  + self.sep_coeff[region] * self.orbit.flare_intensity_integral(t0, t1)) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # ECC memory with persistent flipped-bit state # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- @dataclass(frozen=True) class ScrubResult:     corrected_words: int = 0     due_words: int = 0     sdc_words: int = 0 class EccMemory:     """Sparse flipped-bit set + bit-interleaved SECDED word mapping.     Physical layout: a row holds ``interleave`` words of ``word_bits`` bits, column-interleaved,     so physical column c belongs to word (c mod interleave).  Adjacent physical bits therefore land     in different logical words, which is exactly why interleaving defeats multi-bit upsets <= interleave.     """     def __init__(self, word_bits: int = 64, interleave: int = 4, ecc: bool = True,                  odd_miscorrect_prob: float = 1.0):         self.W, self.I, self.ecc = int(word_bits), int(interleave), bool(ecc)         self.p_mis = float(odd_miscorrect_prob)         self.state = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)     def word_of(self, bits: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:         bits = np.asarray(bits, dtype=np.int64)         row = bits // (self.I * self.W)         col = bits % (self.I * self.W)         return row * self.I + (col % self.I)     def inject(self, positions) -> None:         """XOR-toggle bits: a bit hit twice returns to its correct value."""         pos = np.asarray(positions, dtype=np.int64).ravel()         if pos.size == 0:             return         allp = np.concatenate([self.state, pos])         u, c = np.unique(allp, return_counts=True)         self.state = u[(c % 2) == 1]     @property     def n_flipped(self) -> int:         return int(self.state.size)     def clear(self) -> None:         self.state = np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)     def scrub(self, rng: Optional[np.random.Generator] = None) -> ScrubResult:         if self.state.size == 0:             return ScrubResult()         _, counts = np.unique(self.word_of(self.state), return_counts=True)         self.clear()         if not self.ecc:             return ScrubResult(0, 0, int(counts.size))       # every corrupted word is silently wrong         corrected = int(np.sum(counts == 1))         odd_multi = int(np.sum((counts >= 3) & (counts % 2 == 1)))         even_multi = int(np.sum((counts >= 2) & (counts % 2 == 0)))         if odd_multi:             rng = rng or np.random.default_rng(0)             sdc = int(rng.binomial(odd_multi, self.p_mis))         else:             sdc = 0         due = (even_multi) + (odd_multi - sdc)         return ScrubResult(corrected, due, sdc) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # Ledger and immutable statistics # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- @dataclass class RadiationLedger:     n_seu_sram: int = 0     n_mbu: int = 0     n_seu_reg: int = 0     n_set_hits: int = 0     n_set_latched: int = 0     n_sel: int = 0     words_corrected: int = 0     words_due: int = 0     sdc_sram_candidates: int = 0     sdc_sram: int = 0     sdc_register: int = 0     sdc_logic: int = 0     downtime_s: float = 0.0     destroyed: bool = False     destroyed_at_s: Optional[float] = None @dataclass(frozen=True) class RadiationStats:     elapsed_s: float     n_seu_sram: int     n_mbu: int     n_seu_reg: int     n_set_hits: int     n_set_latched: int     n_sel: int     words_corrected: int     words_due: int     sdc_sram_candidates: int     sdc_sram: int     sdc_register: int     sdc_logic: int     downtime_s: float     destroyed: bool     @property     def sdc_total(self) -> int:         return self.sdc_sram + self.sdc_register + self.sdc_logic     @property     def _hours(self) -> float:         return self.elapsed_s / 3600.0     @property     def sdc_per_hr(self) -> float:         return self.sdc_total / self._hours if self._hours > 0 else 0.0     @property     def due_per_hr(self) -> float:         return self.words_due / self._hours if self._hours > 0 else 0.0     def sdc_per_hr_ci(self, conf: float = 0.95):         return poisson_rate_ci(self.sdc_total, self._hours, conf)     @property     def fit_sdc(self) -> float:         """Failures-in-time: SDC events per 1e9 device-hours."""         return self.sdc_per_hr * 1e9     @property     def availability(self) -> float:         return 1.0 - min(1.0, self.downtime_s / self.elapsed_s) if self.elapsed_s > 0 else 1.0     def p_no_sdc(self, mission_hours: float) -> float:         """P(no SDC during mission_hours) under a constant-rate Poisson model (point estimate)."""         return math.exp(-self.sdc_per_hr * mission_hours)     def p_no_sdc_ci(self, mission_hours: float, conf: float = 0.95):         lo, hi = self.sdc_per_hr_ci(conf)         return math.exp(-hi * mission_hours), math.exp(-lo * mission_hours) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # The subsystem that exposes the chip to the environment # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- class RadiationSubsystem:     """Needs only ``env.expected(region, n, t0, t1)``, so tests can inject a stub environment."""     def __init__(self, cfg: SimConfig, env, rng: np.random.Generator):         self.cfg, self.rc, self.env, self.rng = cfg, cfg.radiation, env, rng         rc = self.rc         A = cfg.compute.array_size         self.sram_bits_total = int(cfg.memory.onchip_sram_mb * 2**20 * 8)         self.reg_bits = A * A * rc.reg_bits_per_pe         self.gates = A * A * rc.gates_per_pe         self.mem = EccMemory(rc.ecc_word_bits, rc.ecc_interleave, cfg.memory.ecc_enabled,                              rc.secded_odd_miscorrect_prob)         self.ledger = RadiationLedger()         self._tmr = rc.tmr_residual if rc.tmr_enabled else 1.0     # ---- helpers -------------------------------------------------------     def _cluster_sizes(self, n: int) -> np.ndarray:         m = self.rc.mbu_max_cluster         return 2 + np.minimum(self.rng.geometric(self.rc.mbu_cluster_geom_p, size=n) - 1, m - 2)     def _binom(self, n: int, p: float) -> int:         return int(self.rng.binomial(n, min(1.0, max(0.0, p)))) if n > 0 else 0     # ---- exposure ----------------------------------------------------------     def expose(self, t0: float, t1: float, live_sram_bits: float, freq_ghz: float) -> None:         if t1 <= t0 or self.ledger.destroyed:             return         rc, led, rng = self.rc, self.ledger, self.rng         live = int(max(1, min(live_sram_bits, self.sram_bits_total)))         # SRAM: primary hits -> MBU clusters -> persistent flipped state         n = int(rng.poisson(self.env.expected("sram", live, t0, t1)))         if n:             led.n_seu_sram += n             prim = rng.integers(0, live, size=n)             is_mbu = rng.random(n) < rc.mbu_prob             n_mbu = int(is_mbu.sum())             led.n_mbu += n_mbu             pos = [prim]             if n_mbu:                 reps = self._cluster_sizes(n_mbu) - 1                 owner = np.repeat(np.arange(n_mbu), reps)                 within = np.arange(int(reps.sum())) - np.repeat(np.cumsum(reps) - reps, reps) + 1                 pos.append(np.minimum(prim[is_mbu][owner] + within, self.sram_bits_total - 1))             self.mem.inject(np.concatenate(pos))         # registers: raw flip-flop upsets, derated by AVF (and TMR)         n = int(rng.poisson(self.env.expected("register", self.reg_bits, t0, t1)))         if n:             led.n_seu_reg += n             led.sdc_register += self._binom(n, rc.avf_register * self._tmr)         # SET in combinational logic: must be latched (pulse width x clock), then not masked         hits = int(rng.poisson(self.env.expected("logic_set", self.gates, t0, t1)))         if hits:             led.n_set_hits += hits             p_cap = min(1.0, rc.set_pulse_width_ps * 1e-12 * freq_ghz * 1e9)             latched = self._binom(hits, p_cap)             led.n_set_latched += latched             led.sdc_logic += self._binom(latched, rc.avf_logic * self._tmr)         # SEL: one device         n = int(rng.poisson(self.env.expected("sel", 1, t0, t1)))         if n:             led.n_sel += n             if rc.sel_protected:                 led.downtime_s += n * rc.sel_recovery_s                 self.mem.clear()                    # power cycle reloads state             elif rng.random() < 1.0 - (1.0 - rc.sel_destructive_prob_unprotected) ** n:                 led.destroyed, led.destroyed_at_s = True, t1     def scrub(self) -> ScrubResult:         res = self.mem.scrub(self.rng)         led = self.ledger         led.words_corrected += res.corrected_words         led.words_due += res.due_words         led.sdc_sram_candidates += res.sdc_words         led.sdc_sram += self._binom(res.sdc_words, self.rc.avf_sram)         return res     def stats(self, elapsed_s: float) -> RadiationStats:         l = self.ledger         return RadiationStats(             elapsed_s=elapsed_s, n_seu_sram=l.n_seu_sram, n_mbu=l.n_mbu, n_seu_reg=l.n_seu_reg,             n_set_hits=l.n_set_hits, n_set_latched=l.n_set_latched, n_sel=l.n_sel,             words_corrected=l.words_corrected, words_due=l.words_due,             sdc_sram_candidates=l.sdc_sram_candidates, sdc_sram=l.sdc_sram,             sdc_register=l.sdc_register, sdc_logic=l.sdc_logic,             downtime_s=l.downtime_s, destroyed=l.destroyed) ``` ### `uchu/faults.py` ```python """Real bit flips on real tensors, and measured architectural vulnerability (AVF). v2 injected additive noise into a 64-element array of zeros.  Here a single bit of a real operand is flipped (two's-complement / IEEE-754 aware) and the GEMM output is compared with the fault-free output, so derating factors come from measurement, not guesswork. """ from __future__ import annotations from dataclasses import dataclass from typing import Tuple import numpy as np from .rng import make_rng from .stats import wilson_ci _UINT = {1: np.uint8, 2: np.uint16, 4: np.uint32, 8: np.uint64} def flip_bit(arr: np.ndarray, flat_index: int, bit: int) -> np.ndarray:     """Return a copy of ``arr`` with bit ``bit`` (0 = LSB) of element ``flat_index`` flipped."""     out = np.ascontiguousarray(arr).copy()     nbits = out.dtype.itemsize * 8     if not 0 <= bit < nbits:         raise ValueError(f"bit {bit} out of range for {out.dtype} ({nbits} bits)")     view = out.reshape(-1).view(_UINT[out.dtype.itemsize])     view[flat_index] ^= _UINT[out.dtype.itemsize](1 << bit)     return out def _gemm(a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:     with np.errstate(all="ignore"):          # flipped exponents legitimately produce inf / NaN         if np.issubdtype(a.dtype, np.integer):             return a.astype(np.int64) @ b.astype(np.int64)         return a.astype(np.float64) @ b.astype(np.float64) def _random_operand(rng, shape, dtype) -> np.ndarray:     dt = np.dtype(dtype)     if np.issubdtype(dt, np.integer):         info = np.iinfo(dt)         return rng.integers(info.min, info.max + 1, size=shape).astype(dt)     return rng.standard_normal(shape).astype(dt) @dataclass(frozen=True) class VulnerabilityResult:     avf: float     ci_low: float     ci_high: float     per_bit_avf: Tuple[float, ...]   # index = bit position, 0 = LSB     n_trials: int     tol: float def measure_gemm_vulnerability(M: int = 32, K: int = 32, N: int = 32, dtype="int8", tol: float = 1e-2,                                trials_per_bit: int = 100, seed: int = 0) -> VulnerabilityResult:     """P(single-bit operand flip changes the GEMM output by more than ``tol`` relative L2 error).     Bit positions are weighted uniformly (every stored bit is equally likely to be hit);     A and B are hit in proportion to their size.  Non-finite outputs count as vulnerable.     """     rng = make_rng(seed, "avf", str(dtype), M, K, N)     dt = np.dtype(dtype)     nbits = dt.itemsize * 8     A, B = _random_operand(rng, (M, K), dt), _random_operand(rng, (K, N), dt)     C = _gemm(A, B)     nc = float(np.linalg.norm(C.astype(np.float64)))     p_a = (M * K) / (M * K + K * N)     per_bit, hits, total = [], 0, 0     for bit in range(nbits):         h = 0         for _ in range(trials_per_bit):             if rng.random() < p_a:                 idx = int(rng.integers(0, M * K))                 C2 = _gemm(flip_bit(A, idx, bit), B)             else:                 idx = int(rng.integers(0, K * N))                 C2 = _gemm(A, flip_bit(B, idx, bit))             d = C2.astype(np.float64) - C.astype(np.float64)             err = float(np.linalg.norm(d)) / max(nc, 1e-300) if np.all(np.isfinite(d)) else np.inf             h += err > tol         per_bit.append(h / trials_per_bit)         hits += h         total += trials_per_bit     lo, hi = wilson_ci(hits, total)     return VulnerabilityResult(hits / total, lo, hi, tuple(per_bit), total, tol) ``` ### `uchu/provenance.py` ```python """Run provenance: everything needed to reproduce a result.""" from __future__ import annotations import platform import subprocess import sys from typing import Optional import numpy as np import scipy from .config import SimConfig MODEL_VERSION = "3.0.0" def git_hash() -> Optional[str]:     try:         return subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "--short", "HEAD"],                                        stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, timeout=2).decode().strip()     except Exception:         return None def provenance(cfg: SimConfig, seed: Optional[int] = None) -> dict:     return {         "model_version": MODEL_VERSION,         "config_hash": cfg.config_hash(),         "seed": cfg.random_seed if seed is None else seed,         "python": sys.version.split()[0],         "numpy": np.__version__,         "scipy": scipy.__version__,         "platform": platform.platform(),         "git": git_hash(),     } ``` ### `uchu/simulator.py` ```python """Single-clock simulator: thermal + orbit + radiation advance together, exactly. v3 fixes vs v2   * ONE authoritative clock (``ChipSimulator.t``); thermal, eclipse, flares, scrubbing and     radiation exposure all use the *actual* elapsed time of each step (v2 advanced orbit by a     nominal-frequency estimate while layers ran at throttled speed).   * run_mission() reaches orbit-scale time (eclipse cycles, flares) with macro-steps; v2 simulated     ~10 s and Monte-Carlo used a single iteration, so it never saw an eclipse or a flare.   * no global RNG: every stream comes from ``rng.make_rng(seed, key...)``.   * ensemble members are independent processes with spawned seeds (v2: threads + global seeding). """ from __future__ import annotations from concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor from dataclasses import dataclass, field from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Sequence import numpy as np from .config import SimConfig, SolarActivity from .memory import PassProfile, WorkloadLayer, profile_pass from .orbit import OrbitalEnvironment from .provenance import provenance from .radiation import RadiationEnvironment, RadiationStats, RadiationSubsystem from .rng import child_seed, make_rng from .stats import poisson_rate_ci from .thermal import DVFSState, LumpedThermalModel, PowerModel class ChipSimulator:     def __init__(self, cfg: SimConfig, seed: Optional[int] = None):         self.cfg = cfg.validate()         self.seed = cfg.random_seed if seed is None else int(seed)         self.orbit = OrbitalEnvironment(cfg.orbital, make_rng(self.seed, "orbit"))         self.power = PowerModel(cfg)         self.thermal = LumpedThermalModel(cfg)         self.env = RadiationEnvironment(cfg, self.orbit)         self.rad = RadiationSubsystem(cfg, self.env, make_rng(self.seed, "radiation"))         self.t = 0.0         self.energy_j = 0.0         self.overtemp_s = 0.0         self.throttled_s = 0.0         self.failed = False         self.fail_reason: Optional[str] = None         self._next_scrub = cfg.radiation.scrub_interval_s     def dvfs(self) -> DVFSState:         return self.power.dvfs_state(self.thermal.T)     def advance(self, dt: float, util: float, live_sram_bits: float, state: DVFSState) -> None:         """Advance the whole chip by ``dt`` seconds at fixed (f, V, util)."""         cfg, rc = self.cfg, self.cfg.radiation         t_end = self.t + dt         while self.t < t_end - 1e-12 and not self.failed:             a, b, ecl = self.orbit.eclipse_segments(self.t, t_end)[0]             seg_end = min(b, self._next_scrub, self.t + cfg.thermal.max_substep_s, t_end)             h = seg_end - self.t             T_new = self.thermal.step(                 h, lambda T: self.power.power_w(T, state.freq_ghz, state.voltage_v, util), ecl)             p_end = self.power.power_w(T_new, state.freq_ghz, state.voltage_v, util)             self.energy_j += p_end * h            # end-point rule == backward-Euler energy balance             if T_new > cfg.power.T_max_k:                 self.overtemp_s += h             if state.throttled:                 self.throttled_s += h             if self.thermal.runaway:                 self.failed, self.fail_reason = True, "thermal_runaway"             self.rad.expose(self.t, seg_end, live_sram_bits, state.freq_ghz)             if self.rad.ledger.destroyed:                 self.failed, self.fail_reason = True, "single_event_latchup_destructive"             self.t = seg_end             if self.t >= self._next_scrub - 1e-12:                 self.rad.scrub()                 self._next_scrub += rc.scrub_interval_s     def finalize(self) -> RadiationStats:         """Classify any still-unscrubbed flipped bits (treated as read at end of run)."""         self.rad.scrub()         return self.rad.stats(self.t) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # Short workload run (pass-level stepping, single clock) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- @dataclass class WorkloadResult:     elapsed_s: float     n_passes: int     dense_flops: float     executed_flops: float     avg_tflops_dense: float     avg_power_w: float     energy_j: float     peak_temperature_k: float     final_temperature_k: float     memory_bound_fraction: float     radiation: RadiationStats     failed: bool     fail_reason: Optional[str]     first_pass_layers: list     provenance: dict = field(default_factory=dict)     @property     def tflops_per_watt(self) -> float:         return self.avg_tflops_dense / self.avg_power_w if self.avg_power_w > 0 else 0.0 def run_workload(cfg: SimConfig, layers: Sequence[WorkloadLayer], n_passes: int = 100,                  seed: Optional[int] = None) -> WorkloadResult:     sim = ChipSimulator(cfg, seed)     layers = list(layers)     dense = execd = mb_time = 0.0     peakT = sim.thermal.T     first = None     done = 0     for _ in range(n_passes):         state = sim.dvfs()         prof = profile_pass(layers, cfg, state.freq_ghz)         first = first or prof.layers         sim.advance(prof.duration_s, prof.util, prof.live_sram_bits, state)         dense += prof.dense_flops         execd += prof.executed_flops         mb_time += prof.memory_bound_fraction * prof.duration_s         peakT = max(peakT, sim.thermal.T)         done += 1         if sim.failed:             break     rad = sim.finalize()     el = max(sim.t, 1e-300)     return WorkloadResult(         elapsed_s=sim.t, n_passes=done, dense_flops=dense, executed_flops=execd,         avg_tflops_dense=dense / el / 1e12, avg_power_w=sim.energy_j / el, energy_j=sim.energy_j,         peak_temperature_k=peakT, final_temperature_k=sim.thermal.T,         memory_bound_fraction=mb_time / el, radiation=rad, failed=sim.failed,         fail_reason=sim.fail_reason, first_pass_layers=list(first or []),         provenance=provenance(cfg, sim.seed)) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # Mission run (macro-steps; reaches eclipse cycles and SEP events) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- @dataclass class MissionResult:     mission_s: float     simulated_s: float     completed: bool     failed: bool     fail_reason: Optional[str]     dense_flops: float     avg_tflops_dense: float     avg_power_w: float     energy_j: float     T_min_k: float     T_max_k: float     T_mean_k: float     overtemp_s: float     throttled_fraction: float     radiation: RadiationStats     availability: float     flare_events: list     series: Dict[str, np.ndarray]     provenance: dict = field(default_factory=dict) def run_mission(cfg: SimConfig, layers: Sequence[WorkloadLayer], mission_s: float, duty: float = 1.0,                 dt_max_s: float = 10.0, seed: Optional[int] = None,                 record_every_s: float = 60.0) -> MissionResult:     if not 0.0 <= duty <= 1.0:         raise ValueError("duty must be in [0, 1]")     sim = ChipSimulator(cfg, seed)     layers = list(layers)     cache: Dict[float, PassProfile] = {}     flops = 0.0     sumT = Tmin = Tmax = None     sumT = 0.0     Tmin, Tmax = sim.thermal.T, sim.thermal.T     rec = {"t": [], "T": [], "f": [], "eclipse": [], "flare": []}     next_rec = 0.0     while sim.t < mission_s - 1e-9 and not sim.failed:         state = sim.dvfs()         key = round(state.freq_ghz, 4)         if key not in cache:             cache[key] = profile_pass(layers, cfg, key)         prof = cache[key]         dt = min(dt_max_s, mission_s - sim.t)         t0 = sim.t         sim.advance(dt, duty * prof.util, prof.live_sram_bits, state)         h = sim.t - t0         flops += duty * h * prof.dense_flops / prof.duration_s         T = sim.thermal.T         sumT += T * h         Tmin, Tmax = min(Tmin, T), max(Tmax, T)         if t0 >= next_rec:             rec["t"].append(t0); rec["T"].append(T); rec["f"].append(state.freq_ghz)             rec["eclipse"].append(sim.orbit.in_eclipse(t0)); rec["flare"].append(sim.orbit.flare_intensity_at(t0))             next_rec += record_every_s     rad = sim.finalize()     el = max(sim.t, 1e-300)     avail = rad.availability     flops *= avail     return MissionResult(         mission_s=mission_s, simulated_s=sim.t, completed=sim.t >= mission_s - 1e-6 and not sim.failed,         failed=sim.failed, fail_reason=sim.fail_reason, dense_flops=flops,         avg_tflops_dense=flops / el / 1e12, avg_power_w=sim.energy_j / el, energy_j=sim.energy_j,         T_min_k=Tmin, T_max_k=Tmax, T_mean_k=sumT / el, overtemp_s=sim.overtemp_s,         throttled_fraction=sim.throttled_s / el, radiation=rad, availability=avail,         flare_events=sim.orbit.flares.events, series={k: np.asarray(v) for k, v in rec.items()},         provenance=provenance(cfg, sim.seed)) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # Ensemble (independent processes, spawned seeds, pooled exact Poisson statistics) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- @dataclass class EnsembleResult:     n_samples: int     total_hours: float     total_sdc: int     total_due: int     sdc_per_hr: float     sdc_per_hr_ci: tuple     due_per_hr: float     per_sample_sdc_per_hr: np.ndarray     n_failed: int     mean_availability: float     provenance: dict def _ensemble_worker(args):     cfg, layers, mission_s, duty, dt_max_s, seed, activity = args     cfg = cfg.copy()     if activity is not None:         cfg.orbital.solar_activity = activity     r = run_mission(cfg, layers, mission_s, duty, dt_max_s, seed)     return (r.radiation.sdc_total, r.radiation.words_due, r.simulated_s, r.failed, r.availability) def run_ensemble(cfg: SimConfig, layers: Sequence[WorkloadLayer], n_samples: int, mission_s: float,                  duty: float = 1.0, dt_max_s: float = 10.0, n_jobs: int = 1,                  base_seed: Optional[int] = None,                  solar_weights: Optional[Dict[SolarActivity, float]] = None) -> EnsembleResult:     base = cfg.random_seed if base_seed is None else int(base_seed)     acts: List[Optional[SolarActivity]] = [None] * n_samples     if solar_weights:         keys = list(solar_weights)         w = np.array([solar_weights[k] for k in keys], float)         w /= w.sum()         for i in range(n_samples):             acts[i] = keys[int(make_rng(base, "scenario", i).choice(len(keys), p=w))]     jobs = [(cfg, list(layers), mission_s, duty, dt_max_s, child_seed(base, "sample", i), acts[i])             for i in range(n_samples)]     if n_jobs == 1:         out = [_ensemble_worker(j) for j in jobs]     else:         with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=n_jobs) as ex:             out = list(ex.map(_ensemble_worker, jobs))     sdc = np.array([o[0] for o in out]); due = np.array([o[1] for o in out])     el = np.array([o[2] for o in out]) / 3600.0     hours = float(el.sum())     ci = poisson_rate_ci(int(sdc.sum()), hours)     return EnsembleResult(         n_samples=n_samples, total_hours=hours, total_sdc=int(sdc.sum()), total_due=int(due.sum()),         sdc_per_hr=float(sdc.sum() / hours) if hours > 0 else 0.0, sdc_per_hr_ci=ci,         due_per_hr=float(due.sum() / hours) if hours > 0 else 0.0,         per_sample_sdc_per_hr=sdc / np.maximum(el, 1e-300), n_failed=int(sum(o[3] for o in out)),         mean_availability=float(np.mean([o[4] for o in out])), provenance=provenance(cfg, base)) ``` ### Calibration package ### `uchu/calibration/__init__.py` ```python """Calibration workflow for the placeholder parameters of Uchu Gijutsu v3.""" from .data import (BeamDataset, BeamPoint, CalibrationData, CalibrationDataError, FlightLog, MbuDataset, PerfMeasurement,                    PowerMeasurement, SepCatalog, ThermalSample, effective_let, load_beam_csv, load_mbu_csv,                    load_perf_csv, load_power_csv, load_sep_csv, load_spectrum_csv, load_thermal_csv) from .fit_hardware import (design_coverage, fit_performance, fit_power_model, fit_thermal_model, suggest_gemm_shapes,                            suggest_power_design) from .fit_radiation import (calibrate_avf, fit_cross_section, fit_mbu, fit_power_law_to_table, fit_sep_statistics,                             flight_update, gamma_scale_posterior, ```python """Calibration data schemas and strict, validated CSV loaders. Every loader rejects malformed input with ``CalibrationDataError`` and a message naming the file, row and column: a calibration silently built on a mis-parsed table is worse than none. """ from __future__ import annotations import csv import math from dataclasses import dataclass, field from pathlib import Path from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Sequence, Tuple from ..config import DType, SpectrumTable class CalibrationDataError(ValueError):     """Raised for malformed, physically impossible or insufficient calibration data.""" MAX_TILT_DEG = 60.0   # the 1/cos(theta) effective-LET law is not trustworthy beyond ~60 deg def effective_let(let_normal: float, tilt_deg: float = 0.0) -> float:     """Cosine-law effective LET.  ``tilt_deg`` = angle between beam and surface normal."""     if not 0.0 <= tilt_deg <= MAX_TILT_DEG:         raise CalibrationDataError(f"tilt {tilt_deg} deg outside [0, {MAX_TILT_DEG}] (cosine law invalid)")     return let_normal / math.cos(math.radians(tilt_deg)) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # Datasets # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- @dataclass(frozen=True) class BeamPoint:     """One beam run.  ``x`` = effective LET [MeV cm2/mg] (ions) or energy [MeV] (protons);     ``fluence_cm2`` = fluence through the DEVICE SURFACE (already cos-corrected for tilt);     ``events`` = observed upsets summed over ``n_units`` identical units (bits / FFs / gates / devices)."""     x: float     fluence_cm2: float     events: int     n_units: float = 1.0 @dataclass(frozen=True) class BeamDataset:     name: str     region: str                 # sram | register | logic_set | sel     kind: str                   # ion | proton     points: Tuple[BeamPoint, ...]     def __post_init__(self):         if self.region not in ("sram", "register", "logic_set", "sel"):             raise CalibrationDataError(f"{self.name}: unknown region '{self.region}'")         if self.kind not in ("ion", "proton"):             raise CalibrationDataError(f"{self.name}: kind must be 'ion' or 'proton'")         for i, p in enumerate(self.points):             if not (math.isfinite(p.x) and p.x > 0):                 raise CalibrationDataError(f"{self.name} point {i}: x must be positive and finite")             if not (math.isfinite(p.fluence_cm2) and p.fluence_cm2 > 0):                 raise CalibrationDataError(f"{self.name} point {i}: fluence must be positive")             if p.events < 0 or int(p.events) != p.events:                 raise CalibrationDataError(f"{self.name} point {i}: events must be a non-negative integer")             if not (math.isfinite(p.n_units) and p.n_units > 0):                 raise CalibrationDataError(f"{self.name} point {i}: n_units must be positive") @dataclass(frozen=True) class PowerMeasurement:     T_k: float     f_ghz: float     v_v: float     util: float     power_w: float @dataclass(frozen=True) class ThermalSample:     """Row i describes the interval ENDING at t_s (backward-looking): mean power and eclipse state over     that interval, and the temperature at t_s.  The first row is the initial condition (power ignored)."""     t_s: float     power_w: float     eclipse: bool     T_k: float @dataclass(frozen=True) class PerfMeasurement:     M: int     K: int     N: int     dtype: str     sparsity: float     freq_ghz: float     latency_s: float @dataclass(frozen=True) class MbuDataset:     name: str     counts: Dict[int, int]          # cluster size (bits flipped per strike) -> number of strikes @dataclass(frozen=True) class SepCatalog:     name: str     obs_span_s: float     durations_s: Tuple[float, ...]     intensities_rel: Tuple[float, ...]    # peak intensity relative to the reference event in the SEP tables @dataclass(frozen=True) class FlightLog:     """Upsets counted by on-board EDAC over known exposure.  ``cfg.orbital`` must describe the flight orbit."""     name: str     region: str     n_units: float     segments: Tuple[Tuple[float, float], ...]          # (t0_s, t1_s) of valid monitoring     events: int     flare_events: Tuple[Tuple[float, float, float], ...] = ()   # (start_s, end_s, intensity) from SEP catalogue @dataclass class AvfRequest:     field: str = "avf_sram"     dtype: str = "int8"     tol: float = 1e-2     trials_per_bit: int = 100     shape: Tuple[int, int, int] = (32, 32, 32) @dataclass class DesignFact:     path: str     value: object     source: str @dataclass class CalibrationData:     env_tables: Dict[str, SpectrumTable] = field(default_factory=dict)     env_source: str = ""     beam: List[BeamDataset] = field(default_factory=list)     mbu: Optional[MbuDataset] = None     sep: Optional[SepCatalog] = None     power: List[PowerMeasurement] = field(default_factory=list)     power_source: str = "power measurements"     thermal: List[ThermalSample] = field(default_factory=list)     thermal_source: str = "thermal telemetry"     perf: List[PerfMeasurement] = field(default_factory=list)     perf_source: str = "performance microbenchmarks"     flight: List[FlightLog] = field(default_factory=list)     avf: List[AvfRequest] = field(default_factory=list)     design_facts: List[DesignFact] = field(default_factory=list) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # CSV loading # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- def _rows(path, required: Sequence[str], optional: Sequence[str] = ()):     path = Path(path)     if not path.exists():         raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path}: file not found")     with open(path, newline="") as fh:         lines = [ln for ln in fh if ln.strip() and not ln.lstrip().startswith("#")]     if not lines:         raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path}: empty")     rd = csv.DictReader(lines)     header = [h.strip() for h in (rd.fieldnames or [])]     missing = [c for c in required if c not in header]     if missing:         raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path}: missing column(s) {missing}; found {header}")     out = []     for i, row in enumerate(rd, start=2):         row = {k.strip(): (v.strip() if v is not None else "") for k, v in row.items() if k}         out.append((i, row))     if not out:         raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path}: no data rows")     return path, out def _num(path, i, row, col, default=None, integer=False):     raw = row.get(col, "")     if raw == "":         if default is not None:             return default         raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path} row {i}: column '{col}' is empty")     try:         v = float(raw)     except ValueError:         raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path} row {i}: column '{col}' = '{raw}' is not a number") from None     if not math.isfinite(v):         raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path} row {i}: column '{col}' is not finite")     if integer:         if v != int(v):             raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path} row {i}: column '{col}' = {raw} must be an integer")         return int(v)     return v def load_beam_csv(path, region: str, kind: str, name: Optional[str] = None) -> BeamDataset:     """ion   : let_mev_cm2_mg, fluence_cm2, events [, tilt_deg, n_units]   (fluence = beam fluence normal to the beam;                it is converted to surface fluence F*cos(theta) and LET to LET/cos(theta))        proton: energy_mev,     fluence_cm2, events [, n_units]"""     xcol = "let_mev_cm2_mg" if kind == "ion" else "energy_mev"     path, rows = _rows(path, [xcol, "fluence_cm2", "events"])     pts = []     for i, r in rows:         x = _num(path, i, r, xcol)         fl = _num(path, i, r, "fluence_cm2")         ev = _num(path, i, r, "events", integer=True)         nu = _num(path, i, r, "n_units", default=1.0)         if kind == "ion":             tilt = _num(path, i, r, "tilt_deg", default=0.0)             x = effective_let(x, tilt)             fl = fl * math.cos(math.radians(tilt))         pts.append(BeamPoint(x, fl, ev, nu))     return BeamDataset(name or Path(path).stem, region, kind, tuple(pts)) def load_power_csv(path) -> List[PowerMeasurement]:     path, rows = _rows(path, ["T_k", "f_ghz", "v_v", "util", "power_w"])     out = []     for i, r in rows:         m = PowerMeasurement(*(_num(path, i, r, c) for c in ("T_k", "f_ghz", "v_v", "util", "power_w")))         if not (m.T_k > 0 and m.f_ghz > 0 and m.v_v > 0 and 0 <= m.util <= 1 and m.power_w > 0):             raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path} row {i}: physically impossible values {m}")         out.append(m)     return out def load_thermal_csv(path) -> List[ThermalSample]:     path, rows = _rows(path, ["t_s", "power_w", "eclipse", "T_k"])     out, last = [], -math.inf     for i, r in rows:         t = _num(path, i, r, "t_s")         if t <= last:             raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path} row {i}: t_s must be strictly increasing")         last = t         ecl = r["eclipse"].lower() in ("1", "true", "yes", "t")         if r["eclipse"].lower() not in ("0", "1", "true", "false", "yes", "no", "t", "f"):             raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path} row {i}: eclipse must be 0/1/true/false")         T = _num(path, i, r, "T_k")         if T <= 0:             raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path} row {i}: T_k must be positive")         out.append(ThermalSample(t, _num(path, i, r, "power_w"), ecl, T))     if len(out) < 10:         raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path}: need >= 10 samples, got {len(out)}")     return out def load_perf_csv(path) -> List[PerfMeasurement]:     path, rows = _rows(path, ["M", "K", "N", "dtype", "sparsity", "freq_ghz", "latency_s"])     valid = {d.label for d in DType}     out = []     for i, r in rows:         if r["dtype"] not in valid:             raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path} row {i}: dtype '{r['dtype']}' not in {sorted(valid)}")         sp = _num(path, i, r, "sparsity")         if sp not in (0.0, 0.5):             raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path} row {i}: sparsity must be 0 or 0.5")         lat = _num(path, i, r, "latency_s")         if lat <= 0:             raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path} row {i}: latency must be positive")         out.append(PerfMeasurement(_num(path, i, r, "M", integer=True), _num(path, i, r, "K", integer=True),                                    _num(path, i, r, "N", integer=True), r["dtype"], sp,                                    _num(path, i, r, "freq_ghz"), lat))     return out def load_spectrum_csv(path) -> SpectrumTable:     path, rows = _rows(path, ["x", "phi_integral"])     x = [_num(path, i, r, "x") for i, r in rows]     ph = [_num(path, i, r, "phi_integral") for i, r in rows]     tab = SpectrumTable(x, ph)     import numpy as np     if not (np.all(np.diff(x) > 0) and np.all(np.diff(ph) <= 0) and min(ph) >= 0 and min(x) > 0):         raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path}: x must be positive ascending and Phi(>x) non-negative, non-increasing")     return tab def load_mbu_csv(path, name: Optional[str] = None) -> MbuDataset:     path, rows = _rows(path, ["cluster_size", "count"])     counts: Dict[int, int] = {}     for i, r in rows:         m = _num(path, i, r, "cluster_size", integer=True)         c = _num(path, i, r, "count", integer=True)         if m < 1 or c < 0:             raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path} row {i}: cluster_size >= 1 and count >= 0 required")         counts[m] = counts.get(m, 0) + c     return MbuDataset(name or Path(path).stem, counts) def load_sep_csv(path, obs_span_s: float, name: Optional[str] = None) -> SepCatalog:     path, rows = _rows(path, ["duration_s", "intensity_rel"])     d = tuple(_num(path, i, r, "duration_s") for i, r in rows)     it = tuple(_num(path, i, r, "intensity_rel") for i, r in rows)     if min(d) <= 0 or min(it) <= 0 or obs_span_s <= 0:         raise CalibrationDataError(f"{path}: durations, intensities and obs_span_s must be positive")     return SepCatalog(name or Path(path).stem, float(obs_span_s), d, it) ``` ### `uchu/calibration/registry.py` ```python """Placeholder registry and provenance ledger. ``SimConfig.calibration`` maps a dotted parameter path to a provenance entry.  ``placeholder_audit`` compares the set of parameters the physics depends on against that ledger, so "which numbers in this run are still guesses?" always has a machine-checkable answer.  ``require_calibrated`` lets a study refuse to run with unresolved placeholders. """ from __future__ import annotations from dataclasses import dataclass, field from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List, Optional import numpy as np from ..config import SimConfig REGIONS = ("sram", "register", "logic_set", "sel") KINDS = ("ion", "proton") XS_PARAMS = ("sigma_sat_cm2", "x_th", "width", "shape") STATUS_RESOLVED = ("measured", "derived", "design_fact", "absorbed") STATUS_ACCEPTED = "accepted_placeholder" class UncalibratedError(RuntimeError):     pass def get_by_path(cfg: Any, path: str):     obj = cfg     for part in path.split("."):         obj = getattr(obj, part)     return obj def set_by_path(cfg: Any, path: str, value) -> None:     *head, last = path.split(".")     obj = cfg     for part in head:         obj = getattr(obj, part)     if not hasattr(obj, last):         raise AttributeError(f"no config parameter '{path}'")     setattr(obj, last, value) def _jsonable(v):     if isinstance(v, (np.floating, np.integer)):         return v.item()     if isinstance(v, (list, tuple)):         return [_jsonable(a) for a in v]     return v def record(cfg: SimConfig, path: str, *, source: str, method: str, status: str = "measured",            value=None, ci: Optional[Iterable[float]] = None, note: str = "") -> None:     if status not in STATUS_RESOLVED + (STATUS_ACCEPTED,):         raise ValueError(f"unknown status '{status}'")     cfg.calibration[path] = {         "source": source, "method": method, "status": status,         "value": _jsonable(value if value is not None else _safe_get(cfg, path)),         "ci95": None if ci is None else [float(c) for c in ci], "note": note} def _safe_get(cfg, path):     try:         v = get_by_path(cfg, path)         return v if isinstance(v, (int, float, str, bool)) else None     except AttributeError:         return None def declare(cfg: SimConfig, path: str, value, source: str, status: str = "design_fact") -> None:     """Set a parameter that is a known design fact (netlist, datasheet, ops decision) and record where it came from."""     set_by_path(cfg, path, value)     record(cfg, path, source=source, method="declared", status=status, value=value) def accept_placeholder(cfg: SimConfig, path: str, reason: str) -> None:     """Explicitly keep a placeholder value, with a reason.  Still reported as unresolved by strict audits."""     record(cfg, path, source="analyst", method="accepted", status=STATUS_ACCEPTED, note=reason) def required_paths(cfg: SimConfig) -> List[str]:     paths: List[str] = []     for r in REGIONS:         for k in KINDS:             if getattr(getattr(cfg.radiation, r), k).sigma_sat_cm2 > 0:      # sigma_sat == 0 => mechanism not modelled                 paths += [f"radiation.{r}.{k}.{p}" for p in XS_PARAMS]     paths += ["radiation.environment", "radiation.shield_mm_al", "radiation.shield_atten_len_mm",               "radiation.mbu_prob", "radiation.mbu_max_cluster", "radiation.mbu_cluster_geom_p",               "radiation.avf_sram", "radiation.avf_register", "radiation.avf_logic",               "radiation.gates_per_pe", "radiation.reg_bits_per_pe", "radiation.set_pulse_width_ps",               "radiation.sel_recovery_s",               "orbital.sep_events_per_year", "orbital.sep_mean_duration_s",               "orbital.sep_intensity_median", "orbital.sep_intensity_log_sigma",               "power.base_power_w", "power.static_frac", "power.clock_frac", "power.interconnect_frac",               "power.leak_temp_coeff", "power.interconnect_extra_exponent",               "thermal.capacitance_j_per_k", "thermal.radiator_area_m2", "thermal.sun_projected_fraction",               "thermal.earth_view_fraction", "thermal.albedo_geometry",               "compute.sparse_speedup_at_50", "compute.pipeline_overlap", "memory.hbm_efficiency"]     if cfg.memory.ecc_enabled:         paths.append("memory.ecc_bw_overhead_pct")     if cfg.radiation.tmr_enabled:         paths.append("radiation.tmr_residual")     return paths @dataclass class AuditResult:     resolved: Dict[str, dict] = field(default_factory=dict)     accepted: Dict[str, dict] = field(default_factory=dict)     unresolved: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)     @property     def n_total(self) -> int:         return len(self.resolved) + len(self.accepted) + len(self.unresolved)     def render(self) -> str:         lines = [f"{len(self.resolved)}/{self.n_total} parameters calibrated or declared, "                  f"{len(self.accepted)} accepted placeholders, {len(self.unresolved)} UNRESOLVED"]         lines += [f"  UNRESOLVED  {p}" for p in self.unresolved]         lines += [f"  accepted    {p}  ({e['note']})" for p, e in self.accepted.items()]         return "\n".join(lines) def placeholder_audit(cfg: SimConfig) -> AuditResult:     res = AuditResult()     for p in required_paths(cfg):         e = cfg.calibration.get(p)         if e is None:             res.unresolved.append(p)         elif e["status"] == STATUS_ACCEPTED:             res.accepted[p] = e         else:             res.resolved[p] = e     return res def require_calibrated(cfg: SimConfig, paths: Optional[Iterable[str]] = None, allow_accepted: bool = False) -> None:     """Raise ``UncalibratedError`` if any of ``paths`` (default: every required path) is still a placeholder."""     a = placeholder_audit(cfg)     wanted = set(paths) if paths is not None else None     bad = [p for p in a.unresolved if wanted is None or p in wanted]     if not allow_accepted:         bad += [p for p in a.accepted if wanted is None or p in wanted]     if bad:         raise UncalibratedError(f"{len(bad)} placeholder parameter(s) unresolved: {sorted(bad)[:8]}"                                 + (" ..." if len(bad) > 8 else "")) ``` ### `uchu/calibration/fit_radiation.py` ```python """Radiation-side calibration.   * ``fit_cross_section``   Poisson maximum-likelihood Weibull sigma(x) from beam-test counts, parametric-bootstrap                             uncertainty (negative-binomial resampling when over-dispersed), deviance goodness-of-fit   * ``fit_mbu``             closed-form MLE of the MBU probability and truncated-geometric cluster-size law   * ``fit_sep_statistics``  SEP-event rate / duration / intensity law from an event catalogue   * spectrum tools          power-law fit to a CREME/OMERE table, installation of orbit-specific tables   * flight update           importance-reweighting of the beam-derived parameter draws by on-orbit EDAC counts   * ``calibrate_avf``       measured architectural vulnerability from real bit-flip fault injection """ from __future__ import annotations import math import warnings from dataclasses import dataclass, field from typing import Dict, Iterable, List, Optional, Sequence, Tuple import numpy as np from scipy import stats from scipy.optimize import least_squares, minimize from ..config import CrossSection, SimConfig, SpectrumParams, SpectrumTable from ..faults import measure_gemm_vulnerability from ..orbit import FlareSchedule,
