OrbitalEnvironment from ..radiation import RadiationEnvironment from ..stats import wilson_ci from .data import BeamDataset, CalibrationDataError, MbuDataset, SepCatalog from .registry import record PARAMS = ("sigma_sat_cm2", "x_th", "width", "shape") # =========================================================================== # 1.  Weibull cross-section MLE # =========================================================================== def _mu(theta: np.ndarray, x: np.ndarray, nF: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:     sig, xth, w, s = theta     z = np.clip((x - xth) / w, 0.0, None)     return sig * (1.0 - np.exp(-np.power(z, s))) * nF def _nll(mu: np.ndarray, k: np.ndarray) -> float:     mu = np.maximum(mu, 1e-300)     return float(np.sum(mu - k * np.log(mu))) def _deviance(mu: np.ndarray, k: np.ndarray) -> float:     mu = np.maximum(mu, 1e-300)     with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):         t = np.where(k > 0, k * np.log(np.maximum(k, 1.0) / mu), 0.0)     return float(2.0 * np.sum(t - (k - mu))) @dataclass class CrossSectionFit:     dataset: str     region: str     kind: str     xs: CrossSection     params: Dict[str, float]     ci95: Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]]     samples: np.ndarray                      # (B, 4) bootstrap draws in natural units [sigma_sat, x_th, width, shape]     deviance: float     dof: int     p_value: float     dispersion: float     overdispersed: bool     n_points: int     n_events: int     fixed: Dict[str, float]     warnings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)     def draw(self, i: int) -> CrossSection:         s = self.samples[i % len(self.samples)]         return CrossSection(float(s[0]), float(s[1]), float(s[2]), float(s[3])) class _Problem:     """Bounds + parametrisation shared by the point fit and every bootstrap refit."""     def __init__(self, x, nF, k, fixed, shape_bounds, x_th_bounds):         self.x, self.nF, self.k, self.fixed = x, nF, k, dict(fixed)         sig_meas = k / nF         sig_max = float(sig_meas.max())         x_first = float(x[k > 0].min())         lo_xth = x_th_bounds[0] if x_th_bounds else float(x.min()) / 20.0         hi_xth = x_first * (1 - 1e-6)         rng_x = float(x.max() - x.min())         self.bounds = {             "sigma_sat_cm2": (0.3 * sig_max, 1e3 * sig_max),             "x_th": (min(lo_xth, hi_xth * 0.999), hi_xth),             "width": (max(0.01 * rng_x, 1e-6), 20.0 * float(x.max())),             "shape": shape_bounds,         }         self.free = [p for p in PARAMS if p not in self.fixed]         self.sig_max, self.x_first, self.rng_x = sig_max, x_first, rng_x     def expand(self, u: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:         th = dict(self.fixed)         th.update({p: math.exp(v) for p, v in zip(self.free, u)})         return np.array([th[p] for p in PARAMS])     def to_u(self, theta: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:         d = dict(zip(PARAMS, theta))         return np.array([math.log(d[p]) for p in self.free])     @property     def ubounds(self):         return [(math.log(self.bounds[p][0]), math.log(self.bounds[p][1])) for p in self.free]     def with_counts(self, k):         c = _Problem.__new__(_Problem)         c.__dict__.update(self.__dict__)         c.k = k         return c     def nll(self, u):         return _nll(_mu(self.expand(u), self.x, self.nF), self.k) def _local(prob: _Problem, u0: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, float]:     r = minimize(prob.nll, u0, method="L-BFGS-B", bounds=prob.ubounds,                  options={"ftol": 1e-13, "gtol": 1e-9, "maxiter": 600})     return r.x, float(r.fun) def _global(prob: _Problem, rng: np.random.Generator, n_random: int = 14) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, float]:     ub = prob.ubounds     lo, hi = np.array([b[0] for b in ub]), np.array([b[1] for b in ub])     starts = []     for sh in (1.0, 2.0, 3.0):         for wf in (0.15, 0.5):             th = {"sigma_sat_cm2": 1.1 * prob.sig_max, "x_th": 0.8 * prob.x_first,                   "width": wf * prob.rng_x, "shape": sh}             th.update(prob.fixed)             th = {p: float(np.clip(th[p], *prob.bounds[p])) for p in PARAMS}             starts.append(prob.to_u(np.array([th[p] for p in PARAMS])))     starts += [rng.uniform(lo, hi) for _ in range(n_random)]     scored = sorted(((prob.nll(u), i) for i, u in enumerate(starts)))[:6]     best_u, best_f = None, math.inf     for _, i in scored:         u, f = _local(prob, np.clip(starts[i], lo, hi))         if f < best_f:             best_u, best_f = u, f     return best_u, best_f def fit_cross_section(ds: BeamDataset, rng: np.random.Generator, n_boot: int = 200,                       fix: Optional[Dict[str, float]] = None, shape_bounds=(0.5, 6.0),                       x_th_bounds: Optional[Tuple[float, float]] = None) -> CrossSectionFit:     pts = ds.points     if len(pts) < 4:         raise CalibrationDataError(f"{ds.name}: need >= 4 beam points, got {len(pts)}")     x = np.array([p.x for p in pts], float)     nF = np.array([p.n_units * p.fluence_cm2 for p in pts], float)     k = np.array([p.events for p in pts], float)     if k.sum() == 0:         raise CalibrationDataError(f"{ds.name}: zero events at every point; only an upper limit exists "                                    f"(sigma < {3.0 / nF.max():.3g} cm2/unit at 95 %), not a Weibull fit")     fixed = dict(fix or {})     prob = _Problem(x, nF, k, fixed, shape_bounds, x_th_bounds)     warn: List[str] = []     n_free = len(prob.free)     if len(np.unique(x)) < n_free + 1:         warn.append(f"only {len(np.unique(x))} distinct x values for {n_free} free parameters: poorly constrained")     u_hat, _ = _global(prob, rng)     theta = prob.expand(u_hat)     mu_hat = _mu(theta, x, nF)     D = _deviance(mu_hat, k)     dof = len(x) - n_free     phi = D / dof if dof > 0 else float("nan")     def bootstrap(draw_counts) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:         thetas, devs = [], []         for _ in range(n_boot):             kb = draw_counts()             if kb.sum() == 0:                 continue             pb = prob.with_counts(kb)             cands = [_local(pb, u_hat), _local(pb, np.clip(u_hat + rng.normal(0, 0.25, len(u_hat)),                                                            [b[0] for b in prob.ubounds], [b[1] for b in prob.ubounds]))]             ub, _ = min(cands, key=lambda c: c[1])             th_b = pb.expand(ub)             thetas.append(th_b)             devs.append(_deviance(_mu(th_b, x, nF), kb))         return np.array(thetas), np.array(devs)     th1, d1 = bootstrap(lambda: rng.poisson(mu_hat).astype(float))     if len(d1) < 0.5 * n_boot:         raise CalibrationDataError(f"{ds.name}: bootstrap degenerate ({len(d1)}/{n_boot} usable draws)")     p_val = float((1 + np.sum(d1 >= D)) / (len(d1) + 1)) if dof > 0 else float("nan")     overdisp = bool(dof > 0 and p_val < 0.05)     samples = th1     if overdisp:         g = max(phi, 1.0 + 1e-6)         warn.append(f"over-dispersed (deviance/dof = {phi:.2f}, bootstrap p = {p_val:.3f}): the Poisson model does not "                     f"explain the scatter (fluence/dosimetry errors? SEL/SEFI contamination?). Intervals widened with a "                     f"negative-binomial resample; investigate before trusting the point estimate.")         th2, _ = bootstrap(lambda: rng.poisson(rng.gamma(np.maximum(mu_hat, 1e-12) / (g - 1.0), g - 1.0)).astype(float))         samples = th2     frac_trans = float(np.mean((mu_hat > 0.02 * theta[0] * nF) & (mu_hat < 0.95 * theta[0] * nF)))     if np.sum((mu_hat > 0.02 * theta[0] * nF) & (mu_hat < 0.95 * theta[0] * nF)) < 3:         warn.append("fewer than 3 points on the rising part of the curve: width/shape poorly constrained")     frac_sat = float(1.0 - math.exp(-max((x.max() - theta[1]) / theta[2], 0.0) ** theta[3]))     if frac_sat < 0.9:         warn.append(f"saturation not reached within the tested range (fitted curve is at {frac_sat:.0%} of sigma_sat at the "                     f"largest tested x): sigma_sat is an extrapolation")     sat_spread = np.percentile(samples[:, 0], 97.5) / max(np.percentile(samples[:, 0], 2.5), 1e-300)     if sat_spread > 3.0:         warn.append(f"sigma_sat 95 % interval spans a factor {sat_spread:.1f}")     at_bound = [p for p, v in zip(PARAMS, theta) if p in prob.free and                 (abs(math.log(v) - math.log(prob.bounds[p][0])) < 1e-3 or abs(math.log(v) - math.log(prob.bounds[p][1])) < 1e-3)]     if at_bound:         warn.append(f"estimate sits on a search bound for {at_bound}: constrain it physically or collect more data")     lo, hi = np.percentile(samples, [2.5, 97.5], axis=0)     return CrossSectionFit(         dataset=ds.name, region=ds.region, kind=ds.kind,         xs=CrossSection(*[float(v) for v in theta]), params=dict(zip(PARAMS, map(float, theta))),         ci95={p: (float(lo[i]), float(hi[i])) for i, p in enumerate(PARAMS)}, samples=samples,         deviance=D, dof=dof, p_value=p_val, dispersion=phi, overdispersed=overdisp,         n_points=len(x), n_events=int(k.sum()), fixed=fixed, warnings=warn) def apply_cross_section_fit(cfg: SimConfig, fit: CrossSectionFit, source: Optional[str] = None) -> None:     reg = getattr(cfg.radiation, fit.region)     setattr(reg, fit.kind, CrossSection(**fit.params))     for p in PARAMS:         status = "derived" if p in fit.fixed else "measured"         record(cfg, f"radiation.{fit.region}.{fit.kind}.{p}", source=source or fit.dataset,                method="Poisson MLE (Weibull) + parametric bootstrap", status=status, value=fit.params[p],                ci=fit.ci95[p], note=f"deviance/dof={fit.dispersion:.2f}, p={fit.p_value:.3f}" + (                    "; OVERDISPERSED" if fit.overdispersed else "")) def cross_section_upper_limit(events: int, fluence_cm2: float, n_units: float = 1.0, conf: float = 0.95) -> float:     """One-sided upper limit on sigma per unit when ``events`` were seen (3 / (nF) for zero events at 95 %)."""     return float(stats.chi2.ppf(conf, 2 * (events + 1)) / 2.0 / (fluence_cm2 * n_units)) # =========================================================================== # 2.  MBU multiplicity # =========================================================================== @dataclass class MbuFit:     dataset: str     p_mbu: float     p_mbu_ci95: Tuple[float, float]     geom_p: float     geom_p_ci95: Tuple[float, float]     max_cluster: int     n_strikes: int     chi2_p_value: float     p_samples: np.ndarray     q_samples: np.ndarray     warnings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list) def _cluster_pmf(q: float, max_cluster: int) -> np.ndarray:     """P(size = m | MBU), m = 2..max_cluster, truncated geometric (matches RadiationSubsystem._cluster_sizes)."""     m = np.arange(2, max_cluster + 1)     pmf = q * (1 - q) ** (m - 2)     pmf[-1] = (1 - q) ** (max_cluster - 2)     return pmf def fit_mbu(ds: MbuDataset, rng: np.random.Generator, max_cluster: Optional[int] = None, n_draw: int = 500) -> MbuFit:     counts = {int(m): int(c) for m, c in ds.counts.items() if c > 0}     N = sum(counts.values())     if N < 20:         raise CalibrationDataError(f"{ds.name}: need >= 20 strikes, got {N}")     n_multi = sum(c for m, c in counts.items() if m >= 2)     if n_multi < 5:         raise CalibrationDataError(f"{ds.name}: only {n_multi} multi-bit strikes; cluster law not identifiable")     M = int(max_cluster or max(counts))     if M < 2:         raise CalibrationDataError("max_cluster must be >= 2")     warn: List[str] = []     if any(m > M for m in counts):         raise CalibrationDataError(f"{ds.name}: observed cluster size > max_cluster={M}")     p = n_multi / N     n_stop = sum(c for m, c in counts.items() if 2 <= m < M)          # geometric "success" events     n_cont = sum(c * (m - 2) for m, c in counts.items() if m >= 2)    # geometric "failure" events     q = n_stop / (n_stop + n_cont) if (n_stop + n_cont) > 0 else 1.0     q = min(max(q, 1e-6), 1.0)     q_ci = wilson_ci(n_stop, n_stop + n_cont) if n_stop + n_cont > 0 else (0.0, 1.0)     exp = np.array([n_multi * v for v in _cluster_pmf(q, M)])     obs = np.array([counts.get(m, 0) for m in range(2, M + 1)], float)     # pool bins with expected < 5 into the last bin     while len(exp) > 2 and exp[-1] < 5:         exp[-2] += exp[-1]; obs[-2] += obs[-1]; exp, obs = exp[:-1], obs[:-1]     dof = len(exp) - 2     pval = float(stats.chi2.sf(np.sum((obs - exp) ** 2 / np.maximum(exp, 1e-12)), dof)) if dof > 0 else float("nan")     if max(counts) == M and counts.get(M, 0) > 0:         warn.append(f"largest observed cluster ({M}) is the truncation point: the tail beyond it is unobserved")     if q_ci[1] - q_ci[0] > 0.4:         warn.append("cluster-size law poorly constrained (few multi-bit strikes)")     return MbuFit(ds.name, p, wilson_ci(n_multi, N), q, q_ci, M, N, pval,                   rng.beta(n_multi + 0.5, N - n_multi + 0.5, size=n_draw),                   rng.beta(n_stop + 0.5, n_cont + 0.5, size=n_draw), warn) def apply_mbu_fit(cfg: SimConfig, fit: MbuFit) -> None:     cfg.radiation.mbu_prob, cfg.radiation.mbu_cluster_geom_p = fit.p_mbu, fit.geom_p     cfg.radiation.mbu_max_cluster = fit.max_cluster     record(cfg, "radiation.mbu_prob", source=fit.dataset, method="binomial MLE + Wilson", value=fit.p_mbu, ci=fit.p_mbu_ci95)     record(cfg, "radiation.mbu_cluster_geom_p", source=fit.dataset, method="truncated-geometric MLE + Wilson",            value=fit.geom_p, ci=fit.geom_p_ci95, note=f"chi2 p={fit.chi2_p_value:.3f}")     record(cfg, "radiation.mbu_max_cluster", source=fit.dataset, method="largest observed cluster", status="derived",            value=fit.max_cluster) # =========================================================================== # 3.  SEP statistics # =========================================================================== @dataclass class SepFit:     dataset: str     n_events: int     rate_per_year: float     rate_ci95: Tuple[float, float]     mean_duration_s: float     duration_ci95: Tuple[float, float]     median_intensity: float     median_ci95: Tuple[float, float]     log_sigma: float     log_sigma_ci95: Tuple[float, float]     samples: np.ndarray            # (n, 4): rate/yr, mean_dur, median, log_sigma     warnings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list) def fit_sep_statistics(cat: SepCatalog, rng: np.random.Generator, n_draw: int = 500, conf: float = 0.95) -> SepFit:     n = len(cat.durations_s)     if n < 3:         raise CalibrationDataError(f"{cat.name}: need >= 3 SEP events, got {n}")     a = 1 - conf     yr = 365.25 * 86400.0     rate = n / cat.obs_span_s * yr     r_lo = 0.5 * stats.chi2.ppf(a / 2, 2 * n) / cat.obs_span_s * yr     r_hi = 0.5 * stats.chi2.ppf(1 - a / 2, 2 * (n + 1)) / cat.obs_span_s * yr     d = np.asarray(cat.durations_s)     dm = float(d.mean())     d_ci = (2 * n * dm / stats.chi2.ppf(1 - a / 2, 2 * n), 2 * n * dm / stats.chi2.ppf(a / 2, 2 * n))     li = np.log(np.asarray(cat.intensities_rel))     mu, sd = float(li.mean()), float(li.std(ddof=1))     tq = stats.t.ppf(1 - a / 2, n - 1) * sd / math.sqrt(n)     s_ci = (sd * math.sqrt((n - 1) / stats.chi2.ppf(1 - a / 2, n - 1)), sd * math.sqrt((n - 1) / stats.chi2.ppf(a / 2, n - 1)))     warn = []     if n < 10:         warn.append(f"only {n} SEP events: rate and intensity law are weakly constrained (heavy tail unobserved)")     # draws: Jeffreys posterior for rate; inverse-gamma for mean duration; t / chi2 for lognormal     rate_d = rng.gamma(n + 0.5, 1.0 / cat.obs_span_s, n_draw) * yr     dur_d = (n * dm) / rng.gamma(n, 1.0, n_draw)     sig_d = sd * np.sqrt((n - 1) / rng.chisquare(n - 1, n_draw))     med_d = np.exp(mu + sig_d / math.sqrt(n) * rng.standard_t(n - 1, n_draw))     return SepFit(cat.name, n, rate, (r_lo, r_hi), dm, d_ci, math.exp(mu), (math.exp(mu - tq), math.exp(mu + tq)),                   sd, s_ci, np.column_stack([rate_d, dur_d, med_d, sig_d]), warn) def apply_sep_fit(cfg: SimConfig, fit: SepFit) -> None:     o = cfg.orbital     o.sep_events_per_year, o.sep_mean_duration_s = fit.rate_per_year, fit.mean_duration_s     o.sep_intensity_median, o.sep_intensity_log_sigma = fit.median_intensity, fit.log_sigma     src = fit.dataset     record(cfg, "orbital.sep_events_per_year", source=src, method="Poisson MLE (Garwood)", value=fit.rate_per_year, ci=fit.rate_ci95)     record(cfg, "orbital.sep_mean_duration_s", source=src, method="exponential MLE", value=fit.mean_duration_s, ci=fit.duration_ci95)     record(cfg, "orbital.sep_intensity_median", source=src, method="lognormal MLE", value=fit.median_intensity, ci=fit.median_ci95)     record(cfg, "orbital.sep_intensity_log_sigma", source=src, method="lognormal MLE", value=fit.log_sigma, ci=fit.log_sigma_ci95) # =========================================================================== # 4.  Environment spectra # =========================================================================== @dataclass class PowerLawFit:     params: SpectrumParams     max_rel_error: float     warnings: List[str] def fit_power_law_to_table(tab: SpectrumTable, x_lo: Optional[float] = None, x_hi: Optional[float] = None,                            x_ref: float = 1.0) -> PowerLawFit:     """Least-squares (log space) fit of Phi(>x) = A[(x/xr)^-g - (xm/xr)^-g] to a table, over [x_lo, x_hi).     Use for sensitivity studies; prefer the table itself (``install_environment_tables``) for the baseline."""     x, ph = np.asarray(tab.x), np.asarray(tab.phi_integral)     x_lo = x_lo or x[0]     x_hi = x_hi or x[-1] * 1.0000001     m = (x >= x_lo) & (x < x_hi) & (ph > 0)     if m.sum() < 4:         raise CalibrationDataError("need >= 4 positive table points in the fitting range")     xs, ys = x[m], np.log(ph[m])     g0 = max(0.1, -np.polyfit(np.log(xs), ys, 1)[0])     A0 = float(np.exp(np.polyval(np.polyfit(np.log(xs), ys, 1), np.log(x_ref))))     def res(v):         A, g = math.exp(v[0]), math.exp(v[1])         model = A * ((xs / x_ref) ** -g - (x_hi / x_ref) ** -g)         return np.log(np.maximum(model, 1e-300)) - ys     r = least_squares(res, [math.log(A0), math.log(g0)])     A, g = math.exp(r.x[0]), math.exp(r.x[1])     err = float(np.max(np.abs(np.exp(res(r.x)) - 1.0)))     w = []     if err > 0.3:         w.append(f"power law misses the table by up to {err:.0%}: use the table directly for baseline rates")     return PowerLawFit(SpectrumParams(A, x_ref, g, float(x_hi)), err, w) def check_table_coverage(cfg: SimConfig, tables: Dict[str, SpectrumTable]) -> List[str]:     """Warn when a table starts above a cross-section threshold (flux below the table is assumed zero)."""     out = []     comp_x = {"gcr_ion": ("ion", "x_th"), "sep_ion": ("ion", "x_th"),               "trapped_proton": ("proton", "x_th"), "sep_proton": ("proton", "x_th")}     for key, tab in tables.items():         kind = comp_x[key][0]         for r in ("sram", "register", "logic_set", "sel"):             xs = getattr(getattr(cfg.radiation, r), kind)             if xs.sigma_sat_cm2 > 0 and tab.x[0] > xs.x_th:                 out.append(f"table '{key}' starts at x={tab.x[0]:.3g}, above {r}.{kind} threshold {xs.x_th:.3g}: "                            f"flux below the table is treated as zero (non-conservative)")     return out def install_environment_tables(cfg: SimConfig, tables: Dict[str, SpectrumTable], source: str) -> List[str]:     """Switch to orbit-specific tabulated spectra.  The tables must already include geomagnetic shielding,     solar-cycle modulation, trapped-belt content and spacecraft shielding; ALL placeholder orbit scalings are bypassed.     The ``sep_*`` tables describe the reference SEP event (intensity = 1)."""     cfg.radiation.environment_mode = "tabulated_orbit"     cfg.radiation.tables = {k: SpectrumTable(list(t.x), list(t.phi_integral)) for k, t in tables.items()}     cfg.validate()     record(cfg, "radiation.environment", source=source, method="orbit-specific tabulated flux (CREME96/OMERE/AP-9)",            status="measured", value="tabulated_orbit", note="tables: " + ",".join(sorted(tables)))     for p in ("radiation.shield_mm_al", "radiation.shield_atten_len_mm"):         record(cfg, p, source=source, method="embedded in tabulated flux", status="absorbed", value=None,                note="shielding applied when the tables were generated")     return check_table_coverage(cfg, tables) # =========================================================================== # 5.  Flight-data update # =========================================================================== def expected_events_for_flight(cfg: SimConfig, region: str, n_units: float,                                segments: Sequence[Tuple[float, float]],                                flare_events: Sequence[Tuple[float, float, float]] = ()) -> float:     orbit = OrbitalEnvironment(cfg.orbital)     orbit.flares = FlareSchedule.from_events(flare_events)     env = RadiationEnvironment(cfg, orbit)     return float(sum(env.expected(region, n_units, t0, t1) for t0, t1 in segments)) @dataclass class FlightUpdate:     log: str     observed: int     expected_prior_mean: float     weights: np.ndarray     ess: float     p_consistency: float     posterior_multiplier_median: float     posterior_multiplier_ci95: Tuple[float, float]     warnings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list) def weighted_quantile(values: np.ndarray, weights: np.ndarray, q: Sequence[float]) -> np.ndarray:     o = np.argsort(values)     v, w = values[o], weights[o]     cw = np.cumsum(w) - 0.5 * w     cw /= w.sum()     return np.interp(q, cw, v) def flight_update(E_draws: np.ndarray, k_obs: int, discrepancy_log_sigma: float = 0.0,                   log: str = "flight", n_gh: int = 41) -> FlightUpdate:     """Importance-reweight parameter draws (each predicting E_j expected events) by the flight count.     ``discrepancy_log_sigma`` is a lognormal model-discrepancy nuisance (environment/geometry errors the beam data     cannot know about); 0 trusts the model exactly."""     E = np.asarray(E_draws, float)     if discrepancy_log_sigma > 0:         z, wz = np.polynomial.hermite_e.hermegauss(n_gh)         delta = np.exp(discrepancy_log_sigma * z)         wz = wz / wz.sum()         mu = E[:, None] * delta[None, :]         like = (stats.poisson.pmf(k_obs, mu) * wz[None, :]).sum(axis=1)         cdf_le = (stats.poisson.cdf(k_obs, mu) * wz).sum(axis=1).mean()         cdf_ge = (stats.poisson.sf(k_obs - 1, mu) * wz).sum(axis=1).mean()     else:         like = stats.poisson.pmf(k_obs, E)         cdf_le, cdf_ge = stats.poisson.cdf(k_obs, E).mean(), stats.poisson.sf(k_obs - 1, E).mean()     p_cons = float(min(1.0, 2.0 * min(cdf_le, cdf_ge)))     w = like / like.sum() if like.sum() > 0 else np.full(len(E), 1.0 / len(E))     ess = float(1.0 / np.sum(w ** 2))     mult = E / np.mean(E)     q = weighted_quantile(mult, w, [0.025, 0.5, 0.975])     warn = []     if p_cons < 0.01:         warn.append(f"flight count {k_obs} is inconsistent with the beam-derived model (prior-predictive p = {p_cons:.2g}): "                     f"suspect environment/shielding/geometry or SEE mechanisms absent from the beam data")     if ess < 0.1 * len(E):         warn.append(f"effective sample size {ess:.0f}/{len(E)}: flight data strongly disagree with most draws; "                     f"increase draws or add a model-discrepancy term")     return FlightUpdate(log, int(k_obs), float(np.mean(E)), w, ess, p_cons, float(q[1]), (float(q[0]), float(q[2])), warn) def gamma_scale_posterior(k_obs: int, E_expected: float, conf: float = 0.95) -> Tuple[float, Tuple[float, float]]:     """Jeffreys Gamma(k + 1/2, E) posterior for a multiplicative rate correction s (k ~ Poisson(s E))."""     d = stats.gamma(k_obs + 0.5, scale=1.0 / E_expected)     a = (1 - conf) / 2     return float(d.mean()), (float(d.ppf(a)), float(d.ppf(1 - a))) # =========================================================================== # 6.  Architectural vulnerability by fault injection # =========================================================================== def calibrate_avf(cfg: SimConfig, field_name: str = "avf_sram", dtype: str = "int8", tol: float = 1e-2,                   trials_per_bit: int = 100, shape: Tuple[int, int, int] = (32, 32, 32), seed: int = 0,                   conservative: bool = True):     """Measure P(output corrupted beyond ``tol`` | single operand bit flip) and store it (upper CI bound if conservative)."""     if field_name not in ("avf_sram",):         raise ValueError("operand-flip injection measures memory (avf_sram) only; register/logic AVF needs RTL "                          "fault injection: use registry.declare() with that result")     r = measure_gemm_vulnerability(*shape, dtype=dtype, tol=tol, trials_per_bit=trials_per_bit, seed=seed)     val = r.ci_high if conservative else r.avf     setattr(cfg.radiation, field_name, float(val))     record(cfg, f"radiation.{field_name}", source=f"GEMM bit-flip injection {dtype} {shape} tol={tol}",            method="Monte-Carlo fault injection + Wilson", value=float(val), ci=(r.ci_low, r.ci_high),            note=("upper 95 % bound used (conservative)" if conservative else "point estimate"))     return r ``` ### `uchu/calibration/fit_hardware.py` ```python """Hardware-side calibration: power model, lumped thermal model, performance (roofline) parameters. Each fit reports parameter covariance from the Jacobian, a condition-number identifiability check on the *measurement design* (non-identifiable designs raise ``CalibrationDataError`` instead of returning confident nonsense), and parameter draws for epistemic-uncertainty propagation. """ from __future__ import annotations import math from dataclasses import dataclass, field from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Sequence, Tuple import numpy as np from scipy.optimize import least_squares from ..config import DType, OrbitType, ProcessNode, SimConfig from ..memory import WorkloadLayer, estimate_layer from ..thermal import SIGMA_SB from .data import CalibrationDataError, PerfMeasurement, PowerMeasurement, ThermalSample from .registry import record COND_WARN, COND_FATAL = 1e4, 1e7 def _covariance(res, n_obs: int, names: Sequence[str], scale: Optional[np.ndarray] = None):     """Wald covariance from the Jacobian.  Identifiability (condition number / weakest direction) is judged on the     Jacobian with each column multiplied by that parameter's plausible range, so it does not depend on units."""     J = res.jac     n_par = J.shape[1]     dof = max(n_obs - n_par, 1)     s2 = float(np.sum(res.fun ** 2) / dof)     Js = J if scale is None else J * np.asarray(scale)[None, :]     sv = np.linalg.svd(Js, compute_uv=False)     cond = float(sv[0] / max(sv[-1], 1e-300))     warn: List[str] = []     if cond > COND_FATAL:         _, _, vt = np.linalg.svd(Js)         weak = names[int(np.argmax(np.abs(vt[-1])))]         raise CalibrationDataError(f"measurement design cannot identify the parameters (condition number {cond:.2g}; "                                    f"weakest direction is dominated by '{weak}'): add measurements that vary it independently")     if cond > COND_WARN:         warn.append(f"ill-conditioned design (condition number {cond:.2g}): some parameters are strongly correlated")     return s2 * np.linalg.pinv(J.T @ J), float(math.sqrt(s2)), warn, cond def _ci(theta: np.ndarray, cov: np.ndarray, lo_hi=None) -> np.ndarray:     se = np.sqrt(np.maximum(np.diag(cov), 0))     return np.column_stack([theta - 1.96 * se, theta + 1.96 * se]) # =========================================================================== # Power model # =========================================================================== @dataclass class PowerFit:     S_w: float     D_w: float     leak_temp_coeff: float     clock_frac: float     config_values: Dict[str, float]     ci95: Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]]     rel_rms_error: float     cond: float     samples: np.ndarray                 # (n, 4) draws of base_power_w, static_frac, leak_temp_coeff, clock_frac     n_points: int     warnings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list) def _power_pred(u, T, f, v, util, cfg, ic_scale):     S, D, k, c = math.exp(u[0]), math.exp(u[1]), u[2], u[3]     ic = cfg.power.interconnect_frac     fr, vr = f / cfg.dvfs.nom_freq_ghz, v / cfg.dvfs.nom_voltage_v     act = (1.0 - c - ic) + ic * ic_scale     return S * np.exp(k * (T - cfg.power.T_nom_k) / cfg.power.T_nom_k) * vr ** 3 + D * fr * vr ** 2 * (c + act * util) def power_to_config_values(cfg: SimConfig, S: float, D: float) -> Tuple[float, float]:     """Invert PowerModel's scaling: (static_nom_w, dynamic_nom_w) -> (base_power_w, static_frac) for THIS chip."""     comp, pw = cfg.compute, cfg.power     area_ratio = comp.area_mm2 / pw.ref_area_mm2     array_ratio = (comp.array_size / pw.ref_array_size) ** 2     ref = ProcessNode[pw.ref_node]     stat = S / (area_ratio * comp.process_node.leakage_factor() / ref.leakage_factor())     dyn = D / (array_ratio * comp.process_node.cap_factor() / ref.cap_factor())     return stat + dyn, stat / (stat + dyn) def fit_power_model(meas: Sequence[PowerMeasurement], cfg: SimConfig, rng: np.random.Generator,                     n_draw: int = 500) -> PowerFit:     if len(meas) < 8:         raise CalibrationDataError(f"need >= 8 power measurements, got {len(meas)}")     a = {n: np.array([getattr(m, n) for m in meas], float) for n in ("T_k", "f_ghz", "v_v", "util", "power_w")}     array_ratio = (cfg.compute.array_size / cfg.power.ref_array_size) ** 2     ic_scale = array_ratio ** cfg.power.interconnect_extra_exponent     c_hi = 0.95 - cfg.power.interconnect_frac     P = a["power_w"]     def resid(u):         return (_power_pred(u, a["T_k"], a["f_ghz"], a["v_v"], a["util"], cfg, ic_scale) - P) / P     lo = np.array([math.log(1e-3), math.log(1e-3), 0.0, 0.0])     hi = np.array([math.log(1e4), math.log(1e4), 12.0, c_hi])     best = None     for S0 in (0.2, 0.5, 0.8):         for k0 in (2.0, 5.0):             u0 = np.array([math.log(S0 * P.mean()), math.log((1 - S0) * P.mean()), k0, 0.1])             r = least_squares(resid, np.clip(u0, lo + 1e-9, hi - 1e-9), bounds=(lo, hi))             if best is None or r.cost < best.cost:                 best = r     cov, sig, warn, cond = _covariance(best, len(P), ["ln S (static)", "ln D (dynamic)", "leak_temp_coeff", "clock_frac"], hi - lo)     u = best.x     at_bound = [n for n, v, l, h in zip(["static", "dynamic", "leak_temp_coeff", "clock_frac"], u, lo, hi)                 if min(v - l, h - v) < 1e-6]     if at_bound:         warn.append(f"estimate at a bound for {at_bound}: Wald interval invalid for those parameters")     draws = rng.multivariate_normal(u, cov, size=n_draw, check_valid="ignore")     draws = np.clip(draws, lo, hi)     S, D = math.exp(u[0]), math.exp(u[1])     base, sf = power_to_config_values(cfg, S, D)     d_base, d_sf = zip(*(power_to_config_values(cfg, math.exp(d[0]), math.exp(d[1])) for d in draws))     samples = np.column_stack([d_base, d_sf, draws[:, 2], draws[:, 3]])     lo_hi = np.percentile(samples, [2.5, 97.5], axis=0)     names = ["base_power_w", "static_frac", "leak_temp_coeff", "clock_frac"]     ci = {n: (float(lo_hi[0, i]), float(lo_hi[1, i])) for i, n in enumerate(names)}     if cfg.power.interconnect_frac > 0:         warn.append("interconnect_frac is not identifiable from chip-level power (it only splits the active power); "                     "supply it from post-layout analysis via registry.declare()")     return PowerFit(S, D, float(u[2]), float(u[3]), dict(zip(names, [base, sf, float(u[2]), float(u[3])])), ci,                     float(np.sqrt(np.mean(best.fun ** 2))), cond, samples, len(P), warn) def apply_power_fit(cfg: SimConfig, fit: PowerFit, source: str) -> None:     for n, v in fit.config_values.items():         setattr(cfg.power, n, v)         record(cfg, f"power.{n}", source=source, method="nonlinear least squares (relative residuals) + Wald/MVN",                value=v, ci=fit.ci95[n], note=f"rms rel. error {fit.rel_rms_error:.3%}, cond {fit.cond:.1e}") def suggest_power_design() -> List[dict]:     """Minimum measurement design that identifies (static, dynamic, leakage slope, clock share)."""     return [{"T_k": T, "f_frac": f, "v_frac": v, "util": u}             for T in (300.0, 340.0, 380.0) for (f, v) in ((1.0, 1.0), (0.6, 0.8)) for u in (0.0, 0.5, 1.0)] # =========================================================================== # Thermal model # =========================================================================== def integrate_thermal(C: float, kr: float, Q_sun: float, Q_ecl: float, t: np.ndarray, P: np.ndarray,                       ecl: np.ndarray, T0: float) -> np.ndarray:     """Backward-Euler / Newton integration of  C dT/dt = P + Q - kr T^4  (same equation as LumpedThermalModel.step,     with constant power over each telemetry interval).  Fast enough for optimiser inner loops."""     T = np.empty(len(t))     T[0] = Tc = T0     for i in range(1, len(t)):         a = C / (t[i] - t[i - 1])         rhs = P[i] + (Q_ecl if ecl[i] else Q_sun)         Tn = Tc         for _ in range(60):             f = a * (Tn - Tc) - rhs + kr * Tn ** 4             step = f / (a + 4.0 * kr * Tn ** 3)             Tn -= step             if abs(step) < 1e-10:                 break         T[i] = Tc = Tn     return T @dataclass class ThermalFit:     C: float     kr: float     Q_sun: float     Q_ecl: float     config_values: Dict[str, float]     ci95: Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]]     rms_K: float     cond: float     samples: np.ndarray        # (n, 4): C, kr, Q_sun, Q_ecl     fixed: List[str]     n_points: int     warnings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list) def thermal_to_config_values(cfg: SimConfig, C: float, kr: float, Q_sun: float, Q_ecl: float) -> Tuple[Dict[str, float], List[str]]:     tc, w = cfg.thermal, []     F_eff = max(0.1, tc.view_factor * (1.0 - tc.degradation_per_year * tc.mission_year))     A = kr / (SIGMA_SB * tc.emissivity * F_eff)     out = {"capacitance_j_per_k": C, "radiator_area_m2": A}     if cfg.orbital.orbit_type == OrbitType.DEEP:         out["sun_projected_fraction"] = max(Q_sun / (tc.absorptance * tc.solar_flux_w_m2 * A), 0.0)         if out["sun_projected_fraction"] > 1.0:             w.append(f"sunlit load implies a projected-area fraction of {out['sun_projected_fraction']:.2f} > 1: telemetry "                      f"contains a heat source the model lacks (self-heating, harness, other units)")         return out, w     Fe = Q_ecl / (tc.emissivity * tc.earth_ir_w_m2 * A)     if Fe > 1.0:         w.append(f"eclipse load implies Earth view fraction {Fe:.2f} > 1: telemetry contains a heat source the model lacks")         Fe = 1.0     alb = tc.absorptance * tc.albedo * tc.solar_flux_w_m2 * Fe * tc.albedo_geometry * A     fsun = (Q_sun - Q_ecl - alb) / (tc.absorptance * tc.solar_flux_w_m2 * A)     if fsun < 0:         w.append("sunlit-minus-eclipse load smaller than the albedo term: sun_projected_fraction clamped to 0")         fsun = 0.0     if fsun > 1.0:         w.append(f"sunlit load implies a projected-area fraction of {fsun:.2f} > 1: telemetry contains a heat source the model lacks")     out.update(earth_view_fraction=Fe, sun_projected_fraction=fsun)     return out, w def fit_thermal_model(samples: Sequence[ThermalSample], cfg: SimConfig, rng: np.random.Generator,                       n_draw: int = 500) -> ThermalFit:     if len(samples) < 20:         raise CalibrationDataError(f"need >= 20 thermal telemetry samples, got {len(samples)}")     t = np.array([s.t_s for s in samples]); P = np.array([s.power_w for s in samples])     ecl = np.array([s.eclipse for s in samples], bool); Tm = np.array([s.T_k for s in samples])     tc = cfg.thermal     F_eff = max(0.1, tc.view_factor * (1.0 - tc.degradation_per_year * tc.mission_year))     from ..thermal import LumpedThermalModel     lm = LumpedThermalModel(cfg)     x0 = np.array([tc.capacitance_j_per_k, tc.emissivity * SIGMA_SB * F_eff * tc.radiator_area_m2,                    lm.env_load_w(False), lm.env_load_w(True)])     names = ["C", "kr", "Q_sun", "Q_ecl"]     fixed: List[str] = []     warn: List[str] = []     has_ecl, has_sun = ecl[1:].any(), (~ecl[1:]).any()     if not has_ecl:         fixed.append("Q_ecl"); warn.append("no eclipse samples: Q_ecl kept at its placeholder (unresolved)")     if not has_sun:         fixed.append("Q_sun"); warn.append("no sunlit samples: Q_sun kept at its placeholder (unresolved)")     free = [i for i, n in enumerate(names) if n not in fixed]     lo = np.array([math.log(1.0), math.log(1e-13), 0.0, 0.0]); hi = np.array([math.log(1e7), math.log(1e-6), 5e3, 5e3])     def full(v):         u = np.array([math.log(x0[0]), math.log(x0[1]), x0[2], x0[3]])         u[free] = v         return u     def model(v):         u = full(v)         return integrate_thermal(math.exp(u[0]), math.exp(u[1]), u[2], u[3], t, P, ecl, Tm[0])     def resid(v):         return model(v) - Tm     best = None     base_u = np.array([math.log(x0[0]), math.log(x0[1]), x0[2], x0[3]])     for fc in (0.3, 1.0, 3.0):         for fk in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):             u0 = base_u.copy(); u0[0] += math.log(fc); u0[1] += math.log(fk)             u0[2:] = np.maximum(u0[2:], 1.0)             r = least_squares(resid, np.clip(u0[free], lo[free] + 1e-9, hi[free] - 1e-9),                               bounds=(lo[free], hi[free]), x_scale="jac")             if best is None or r.cost < best.cost:                 best = r     cov_f, sig, w2, cond = _covariance(best, len(Tm) - 1, [names[i] for i in free], (hi - lo)[free])     warn += w2     u = full(best.x)     cov = np.zeros((4, 4)); idx = np.array(free)     cov[np.ix_(idx, idx)] = cov_f     d = rng.multivariate_normal(u, cov, size=n_draw, check_valid="ignore")     d = np.clip(d, lo, hi)     samples_nat = np.column_stack([np.exp(d[:, 0]), np.exp(d[:, 1]), d[:, 2], d[:, 3]])     C, kr, Qs, Qe = math.exp(u[0]), math.exp(u[1]), float(u[2]), float(u[3])     cfgv, w3 = thermal_to_config_values(cfg, C, kr, Qs, Qe)     warn += w3     keys = list(cfgv)     mapped = np.array([[thermal_to_config_values(cfg, *row)[0][k] for k in keys] for row in samples_nat])     qlo, qhi = np.percentile(mapped, [2.5, 97.5], axis=0)     ci = {k: (float(qlo[i]), float(qhi[i])) for i, k in enumerate(keys)}     return ThermalFit(C, kr, Qs, Qe, cfgv, ci, float(np.sqrt(np.mean(best.fun ** 2))), cond,                       samples_nat, fixed, len(Tm), warn) def apply_thermal_fit(cfg: SimConfig, fit: ThermalFit, source: str) -> None:     for k, v in fit.config_values.items():         if (k == "earth_view_fraction" and "Q_ecl" in fit.fixed) or (k == "sun_projected_fraction" and "Q_sun" in fit.fixed):             continue         setattr(cfg.thermal, k, v)         record(cfg, f"thermal.{k}", source=source, method="Newton/backward-Euler trajectory fit + Wald/MVN",                status="derived" if k in ("earth_view_fraction", "sun_projected_fraction") else "measured",                value=v, ci=fit.ci95[k], note=f"telemetry rms {fit.rms_K:.3f} K, cond {fit.cond:.1e}")     record(cfg, "thermal.albedo_geometry", source=source, method="not separable from sun_projected_fraction",            status="absorbed", value=cfg.thermal.albedo_geometry, note="sunlit load fitted as a lump; geometry split is nominal") # =========================================================================== # Performance (roofline) parameters # =========================================================================== _DT = {d.label: d for d in DType} @dataclass class PerfFit:     params: Dict[str, float]     ci95: Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]]     t_launch_s: float     log_rms: float     cond: float     samples: np.ndarray            # (n, 3): overlap, hbm_efficiency, speedup     fixed: List[str]     informative: Dict[str, int]     n_points: int     warnings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list) def _layer(m: PerfMeasurement) -> WorkloadLayer:     return WorkloadLayer("m", "gemm", m.M, m.K, m.N, _DT[m.dtype], m.sparsity) def _set(cfg: SimConfig, overlap: float, eff: float, spd: float) -> None:     cfg.compute.pipeline_overlap, cfg.memory.hbm_efficiency, cfg.compute.sparse_speedup_at_50 = overlap, eff, spd def regime_counts(meas: Sequence[PerfMeasurement], cfg: SimConfig) -> Dict[str, int]:     """How many measurements are informative about each parameter (evaluated under extreme settings)."""     c = cfg.copy()     n = {"pipeline_overlap": 0, "hbm_efficiency": 0, "sparse_speedup_at_50": 0}     for m in meas:         L = _layer(m)         _set(c, 0.0, 1.0, cfg.compute.sparse_speedup_at_50)         a = estimate_layer(L, c, m.freq_ghz)         tiles = math.ceil(m.M / c.compute.array_size) * math.ceil(m.N / c.compute.array_size)         fill = (2 * c.compute.array_size - 2) * tiles         cycles_total = a.t_compute_s * m.freq_ghz * 1e9         if tiles > 1 and fill / cycles_total > 0.15 and a.t_memory_s < a.t_compute_s:             n["pipeline_overlap"] += 1         if a.t_memory_s > 0.3 * a.t_compute_s:             n["hbm_efficiency"] += 1         if m.sparsity == 0.5 and a.t_memory_s < 0.5 * a.t_compute_s:             n["sparse_speedup_at_50"] += 1     return n def fit_performance(meas: Sequence[PerfMeasurement], cfg: SimConfig, rng: np.random.Generator,                     n_draw: int = 500, min_informative: int = 3) -> PerfFit:     if len(meas) < 8:         raise CalibrationDataError(f"need >= 8 latency measurements, got {len(meas)}")     info = regime_counts(meas, cfg)     names = ["pipeline_overlap", "hbm_efficiency", "sparse_speedup_at_50"]     cur = [cfg.compute.pipeline_overlap, cfg.memory.hbm_efficiency, cfg.compute.sparse_speedup_at_50]     fixed = [n for n in names if info[n] < min_informative]     warn = [f"{n}: only {info[n]} informative measurement(s) (< {min_informative}); kept at its placeholder (unresolved)"             for n in fixed]     free = [i for i, n in enumerate(names) if n not in fixed]     lat = np.array([m.latency_s for m in meas])     lo = np.array([0.0, 0.05, 1.0, 0.0]); hi = np.array([1.0, 1.0, 2.0, 0.999 * lat.min()])      # launch overhead cannot exceed the fastest measurement     c = cfg.copy()     layers = [_layer(m) for m in meas]     def predict(v):         p = np.array(cur + [0.0], float)         p[free] = v[:len(free)]         p[3] = v[-1]         _set(c, p[0], p[1], p[2])         return np.array([estimate_layer(L, c, m.freq_ghz, exact_cycles=False).duration_s for L, m in zip(layers, meas)]) + p[3]     def resid(v):         return np.log(predict(v)) - np.log(lat)     def solve(free_):         idx_ = free_ + [3]         best_ = None         for o0 in (0.2, 0.8):             for e0 in (0.4, 0.9):                 for s0 in (1.4, 1.9):                     p0 = np.array([o0, e0, s0, 0.1 * lat.min()])                     r = least_squares(lambda v: resid_for(free_, v), np.clip(p0[idx_], lo[idx_] + 1e-9, hi[idx_] - 1e-9),                                       bounds=(lo[idx_], hi[idx_]), x_scale="jac")                     if best_ is None or r.cost < best_.cost:                         best_ = r         return best_     def resid_for(free_, v):         p = np.array(cur + [0.0], float)         p[free_] = v[:len(free_)]         p[3] = v[-1]         _set(c, p[0], p[1], p[2])         pred = np.array([estimate_layer(L, c, m.freq_ghz, exact_cycles=False).duration_s for L, m in zip(layers, meas)]) + p[3]         return np.log(pred) - np.log(lat)     best = solve(free)     while True:                       # drop parameters with no sensitivity at the optimum (non-binding regime)         cn = np.linalg.norm(best.jac, axis=0) * (hi - lo)[free + [3]]      # sensitivity over the plausible range         weak = [free[j] for j in range(len(free)) if cn[j] < 1e-6 * max(cn.max(), 1e-300)]         if not weak:             break         for i_ in weak:             fixed.append(names[i_])             warn.append(f"{names[i_]}: zero sensitivity at the optimum (that regime is not binding on this chip); "                         f"kept at its placeholder (unresolved)")         free = [i_ for i_ in free if i_ not in weak]         if not free:             raise CalibrationDataError("no performance parameter is identifiable from these measurements")         best = solve(free)     idx = free + [3]     cov_f, sig, w2, cond = _covariance(best, len(lat), [names[i] for i in free] + ["t_launch"], (hi - lo)[free + [3]])     warn += w2     est = np.array(cur + [0.0], float); est[free] = best.x[:len(free)]; est[3] = best.x[-1]     cov = np.zeros((4, 4)); ix = np.array(idx); cov[np.ix_(ix, ix)] = cov_f     d = np.clip(rng.multivariate_normal(est, cov, size=n_draw, check_valid="ignore"), lo, hi)     for j in range(3):         if j not in free:             d[:, j] = est[j]     q = np.percentile(d[:, :3], [2.5, 97.5], axis=0)     ci = {n: (float(q[0, i]), float(q[1, i])) for i, n in enumerate(names)}     at_bound = [names[i] for i in free if min(est[i] - lo[i], hi[i] - est[i]) < 1e-6]     if est[3] > 0.99 * hi[3]:         at_bound.append("t_launch")     if at_bound:         warn.append(f"estimate at a physical bound for {at_bound}: Wald interval invalid for those parameters")     if cfg.memory.ecc_enabled:         warn.append("hbm_efficiency absorbs the ECC bandwidth overhead (not separable from achieved-bandwidth efficiency)")     return PerfFit(dict(zip(names, map(float, est[:3]))), ci, float(est[3]), float(np.sqrt(np.mean(best.fun ** 2))),                    cond, d[:, :3], fixed, info, len(lat), warn) def apply_perf_fit(cfg: SimConfig, fit: PerfFit, source: str) -> None:     for n, v in fit.params.items():         if n in fit.fixed:             continue         sect = cfg.memory if n == "hbm_efficiency" else cfg.compute         setattr(sect, n, v)         record(cfg, ("memory." if n == "hbm_efficiency" else "compute.") + n, source=source,                method="log-latency least squares + Wald/MVN", value=v, ci=fit.ci95[n],                note=f"log rms {fit.log_rms:.3f}, informative points {fit.informative[n]}")     if cfg.memory.ecc_enabled and "hbm_efficiency" not in fit.fixed:         record(cfg, "memory.ecc_bw_overhead_pct", source=source, method="absorbed into hbm_efficiency", status="absorbed",                value=cfg.memory.ecc_bw_overhead_pct) def suggest_gemm_shapes(cfg: SimConfig) -> List[dict]:     """Microbenchmark set spanning the regimes the three performance parameters are sensitive to.     Check it against YOUR chip with ``design_coverage`` before spending bench time."""     A = cfg.compute.array_size     out = []     for K in (A // 4, A // 2, A):                                  # shallow-K, many tiles -> pipeline_overlap         out.append(dict(M=8 * A, K=K, N=8 * A, dtype="int8", sparsity=0.0))     for M in (1, 8, 32, 128):                                       # skinny / weight-streaming -> hbm_efficiency         out.append(dict(M=M, K=16 * A, N=16 * A, dtype="int8", sparsity=0.0))     for K in (32 * A, 96 * A, 160 * A):                             # large, deep, compute-bound dense+sparse pairs         for sp in (0.0, 0.5):             out.append(dict(M=16 * A, K=K, N=16 * A, dtype="int8", sparsity=sp))     for s in (A // 2, A):                                           # tiny: fixed launch overhead         out.append(dict(M=s, K=s, N=s, dtype="int8", sparsity=0.0))     return out def design_coverage(cfg: SimConfig, shapes: Optional[Sequence[dict]] = None, freq_ghz: Optional[float] = None,                     min_informative: int = 3) -> Dict[str, int]:     """Number of benchmark shapes informative about each performance parameter on this chip (0 => unidentifiable)."""     shapes = list(shapes if shapes is not None else suggest_gemm_shapes(cfg))     f = freq_ghz or cfg.dvfs.nom_freq_ghz     meas = [PerfMeasurement(s["M"], s["K"], s["N"], s["dtype"], s["sparsity"], f, 1.0) for s in shapes]     return regime_counts(meas, cfg) ``` ### `uchu/calibration/workflow.py` ```python """Calibration workflow orchestrator.     data  --->  [design facts] -> [environment tables] -> [beam: cross-sections] -> [MBU] -> [SEP] -> [power]                 -> [thermal] -> [performance] -> [AVF] -> [joint epistemic draws + flight reweighting] -> [audit] Design rules   * every step is isolated: bad or insufficient data in one step records a FAILED step and leaves those parameters     UNRESOLVED; it never aborts the others and never silently substitutes a guess   * every fitted parameter is written to ``cfg.calibration`` (source, method, value, 95 % interval, notes)   * epistemic uncertainty is carried as joint parameter draws that can be pushed through the simulator     (``sample_configs`` / ``epistemic_mission_study``) -- not left as a table of intervals """ from __future__ import annotations import argparse import json import sys import time from dataclasses import dataclass, field from pathlib import Path from typing import Callable, Dict, List, Optional, Sequence, Tuple import numpy as np from ..config import CrossSection, SimConfig from ..orbit import FlareSchedule, OrbitalEnvironment from ..provenance import provenance from ..radiation import RadiationEnvironment from ..rng import child_seed, make_rng from . import data as D from .data import CalibrationData, CalibrationDataError from .fit_hardware import (apply_perf_fit, apply_power_fit, apply_thermal_fit, fit_performance, fit_power_model,                            fit_thermal_model, thermal_to_config_values) from .fit_radiation import (apply_cross_section_fit, apply_mbu_fit, apply_sep_fit, calibrate_avf, expected_events_for_flight,                             fit_cross_section, fit_mbu, fit_sep_statistics, flight_update, install_environment_tables,                             weighted_quantile) from .registry import AuditResult, declare, placeholder_audit, set_by_path @dataclass class CalibrationOptions:     seed: int = 0     n_boot: int = 200     n_draws: int = 200     flight_discrepancy_log_sigma: float = 0.0     min_perf_informative: int = 3 @dataclass class StepResult:     name: str     status: str                          # ok | skipped | failed     summary: dict = field(default_factory=dict)     warnings: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)     error: Optional[str] = None def _f(x):     return float(x) if isinstance(x, (int, float, np.floating, np.integer)) else x def _ci(c):     return [float(c[0]), float(c[1])] # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- @dataclass class CalibrationReport:     config: SimConfig     initial_hash: str     steps: List[StepResult]     audit: AuditResult     options: CalibrationOptions     fits: Dict[str, object]     draws: List[Dict[str, object]]     weights: np.ndarray     flight: list     rate_summary: dict     provenance: dict     # ---- epistemic propagation ------------------------------------------------     def config_for_draw(self, j: int) -> SimConfig:         c = self.config.copy()         for path, val in self.draws[j].items():             set_by_path(c, path, CrossSection(**vars(val)) if isinstance(val, CrossSection) else val)         return c.validate()     def sample_configs(self, n: int, rng: np.random.Generator) -> List[SimConfig]:         """Configs drawn from the (flight-reweighted) epistemic posterior over all fitted parameters."""         idx = rng.choice(len(self.draws), size=n, p=self.weights / self.weights.sum())         return [self.config_for_draw(int(j)) for j in idx]     # ---- serialisation -------------------------------------------------------     def to_dict(self) -> dict:         return {             "provenance": self.provenance, "initial_config_hash": self.initial_hash,             "calibrated_config_hash": self.config.config_hash(),             "options": vars(self.options),             "steps": [vars(s) for s in self.steps],             "ledger": self.config.calibration,             "audit": {"resolved": sorted(self.audit.resolved), "accepted": sorted(self.audit.accepted),                       "unresolved": self.audit.unresolved},             "flight": [{k: (v.tolist() if isinstance(v, np.ndarray) else v) for k, v in vars(fu).items() if k != "weights"}                        for fu in self.flight],             "rate_summary": self.rate_summary,         }     def to_markdown(self) -> str:         a = self.audit         L = ["# Calibration report", "",              f"*model {self.provenance['model_version']} · seed {self.options.seed} · "              f"config `{self.initial_hash}` → `{self.config.config_hash()}`*", "",              f"**{len(a.resolved)}/{a.n_total} required parameters calibrated or declared · "              f"{len(a.accepted)} accepted placeholders · {len(a.unresolved)} UNRESOLVED**", "", "## Steps", "",              "| step | status | notes |", "|---|---|---|"]         for s in self.steps:             note = (s.error or "; ".join(s.warnings[:2]) or "")[:140].replace("|", "/")             L.append(f"| {s.name} | {s.status} | {note} |")         L += ["", "## Calibrated parameters", "", "| parameter | value | 95 % interval | status | source · method |", "|---|---|---|---|---|"]         for p, e in sorted(self.config.calibration.items()):             v = e["value"]             vs = f"{v:.4g}" if isinstance(v, (int, float)) else str(v)             ci = "" if not e["ci95"] else f"[{e['ci95'][0]:.4g}, {e['ci95'][1]:.4g}]"             L.append(f"| `{p}` | {vs} | {ci} | {e['status']} | {e['source']} · {e['method']} |")         if self.rate_summary:             L += ["", "## Predicted per-bit upset rate (orbit-static, per bit per day)", "",                   "| region | placeholder | calibrated (95 %) | after flight data (95 %) |", "|---|---|---|---|"]             for r, d in self.rate_summary.items():                 post = d.get("posterior")                 flag = (f" ⚠ {d['unresolved_fraction']:.0%} rests on placeholder {'/'.join(d['unresolved_kinds'])} cross-section"                         if d.get("unresolved_fraction", 0) > 0.05 else "")                 L.append(f"| {r}{flag} | {d['placeholder']:.3g} | {d['median']:.3g} [{d['lo']:.3g}, {d['hi']:.3g}] | "                          + (f"{post['median']:.3g} [{post['lo']:.3g}, {post['hi']:.3g}]" if post else "n/a") + " |")         if self.flight:             L += ["", "## Flight-data consistency", "", "| log | observed | prior-expected | p(consistent) | posterior multiplier | ESS |", "|---|---|---|---|---|---|"]             for f in self.flight:                 L.append(f"| {f.log} | {f.observed} | {f.expected_prior_mean:.1f} | {f.p_consistency:.3g} | "                          f"{f.posterior_multiplier_median:.2f} [{f.posterior_multiplier_ci95[0]:.2f}, {f.posterior_multiplier_ci95[1]:.2f}] | {f.ess:.0f} |")         warns = [(s.name, w) for s in self.steps for w in s.warnings]         if warns:             L += ["", "## Warnings", ""] + [f"- **{n}**: {w}" for n, w in warns]         if a.unresolved or a.accepted:             L += ["", "## Still placeholders", ""] + [f"- UNRESOLVED `{p}`" for p in a.unresolved] + \                  [f"- accepted `{p}` — {e['note']}" for p, e in a.accepted.items()]         return "\n".join(L) + "\n"     def save(self, out_dir) -> Path:         out = Path(out_dir); out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)         (out / "calibrated_config.json").write_text(self.config.to_json())         (out / "calibration_report.json").write_text(json.dumps(self.to_dict(), indent=2, default=str))         (out / "calibration_report.md").write_text(self.to_markdown())         return out # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- def _static_rate_per_bit_day(cfg: SimConfig, region: str) -> float:     orbit = OrbitalEnvironment(cfg.orbital)     orbit.flares = FlareSchedule.from_events([])     return RadiationEnvironment(cfg, orbit).static_rate[region] * 86400.0 def run_calibration(cfg: SimConfig, data: CalibrationData, opts: Optional[CalibrationOptions] = None) -> CalibrationReport:     opts = opts or CalibrationOptions()     initial_hash = cfg.config_hash()     placeholder_cfg = cfg.copy()     cfg = cfg.copy().validate()     steps: List[StepResult] = []     fits: Dict[str, object] = {}     def step(name: str, fn: Callable[[], Tuple[dict, List[str]]], active: bool = True):         if not active:             steps.append(StepResult(name, "skipped")); return         try:             summary, warns = fn()             steps.append(StepResult(name, "ok", summary, list(warns)))         except CalibrationDataError as e:             steps.append(StepResult(name, "failed", error=str(e)))         except Exception as e:                                           # a bug must not look like a data problem             steps.append(StepResult(name, "failed", error=f"INTERNAL {type(e).__name__}: {e}"))     rng = lambda *k: make_rng(opts.seed, "calib", *k)     # 1. declared design facts     def _facts():         for f in data.design_facts:             declare(cfg, f.path, f.value, f.source)         return {"declared": [f.path for f in data.design_facts]}, []     step("design facts", _facts, bool(data.design_facts))     # 2. environment     def _env():         w = install_environment_tables(cfg, data.env_tables, data.env_source or "environment tables")         return {"components": sorted(data.env_tables)}, w     step("environment tables", _env, bool(data.env_tables))     # 3. beam data     for ds in data.beam:         def _beam(ds=ds):             fit = fit_cross_section(ds, rng("xs", ds.name), n_boot=opts.n_boot)             apply_cross_section_fit(cfg, fit)             fits[f"xs:{ds.region}:{ds.kind}"] = fit             return ({"params": {k: _f(v) for k, v in fit.params.items()},                      "ci95": {k: _ci(c) for k, c in fit.ci95.items()}, "deviance_per_dof": _f(fit.dispersion),                      "p_value": _f(fit.p_value), "n_events": fit.n_events, "overdispersed": fit.overdispersed}, fit.warnings)         step(f"beam: {ds.region}/{ds.kind} ({ds.name})", _beam)     # 4. MBU     def _mbu():         fit = fit_mbu(data.mbu, rng("mbu")); apply_mbu_fit(cfg, fit); fits["mbu"] = fit         return {"p_mbu": fit.p_mbu, "p_mbu_ci95": _ci(fit.p_mbu_ci95), "geom_p": fit.geom_p,                 "geom_p_ci95": _ci(fit.geom_p_ci95), "max_cluster": fit.max_cluster, "chi2_p": _f(fit.chi2_p_value)}, fit.warnings     step("MBU multiplicity", _mbu, data.mbu is not None)     # 5. SEP     def _sep():         fit = fit_sep_statistics(data.sep, rng("sep")); apply_sep_fit(cfg, fit); fits["sep"] = fit         return {"n_events": fit.n_events, "rate_per_year": fit.rate_per_year, "rate_ci95": _ci(fit.rate_ci95),                 "median_intensity": fit.median_intensity, "log_sigma": fit.log_sigma}, fit.warnings     step("SEP statistics", _sep, data.sep is not None)     # 6-8. hardware     def _power():         fit = fit_power_model(data.power, cfg, rng("power")); apply_power_fit(cfg, fit, data.power_source); fits["power"] = fit         return {"values": fit.config_values, "ci95": {k: _ci(v) for k, v in fit.ci95.items()},                 "rel_rms": fit.rel_rms_error, "cond": fit.cond}, fit.warnings     step("power model", _power, bool(data.power))     def _thermal():         fit = fit_thermal_model(data.thermal, cfg, rng("thermal")); apply_thermal_fit(cfg, fit, data.thermal_source); fits["thermal"] = fit         return {"values": fit.config_values, "ci95": {k: _ci(v) for k, v in fit.ci95.items()},                 "rms_K": fit.rms_K, "cond": fit.cond, "fixed": fit.fixed}, fit.warnings     step("thermal model", _thermal, bool(data.thermal))     def _perf():         fit = fit_performance(data.perf, cfg, rng("perf"), min_informative=opts.min_perf_informative)         apply_perf_fit(cfg, fit, data.perf_source); fits["perf"] = fit         return {"params": fit.params, "ci95": {k: _ci(v) for k, v in fit.ci95.items()}, "t_launch_s": fit.t_launch_s,                 "unresolved": fit.fixed, "informative": fit.informative, "log_rms": fit.log_rms}, fit.warnings     step("performance", _perf, bool(data.perf))     # 9. AVF     for req in data.avf:         def _avf(req=req):             r = calibrate_avf(cfg, req.field, req.dtype, req.tol, req.trials_per_bit, req.shape, seed=opts.seed)             return {"avf": r.avf, "ci95": [r.ci_low, r.ci_high], "used": getattr(cfg.radiation, req.field)}, []         step(f"AVF ({req.field}, {req.dtype}, tol={req.tol})", _avf)     # 10. joint epistemic draws, flight reweighting     draws: List[Dict[str, object]] = []     weights = np.ones(opts.n_draws)     flight_updates: list = []     rate_summary: dict = {}     def _draws():         nonlocal weights         r = rng("draws")         for _ in range(opts.n_draws):             a: Dict[str, object] = {}             for key, fit in fits.items():                 if key.startswith("xs:"):                     _, region, kind = key.split(":")                     a[f"radiation.{region}.{kind}"] = fit.draw(int(r.integers(len(fit.samples))))                 elif key == "mbu":                     i = int(r.integers(len(fit.p_samples)))                     a.update({"radiation.mbu_prob": float(fit.p_samples[i]), "radiation.mbu_cluster_geom_p": float(max(fit.q_samples[i], 1e-3))})                 elif key == "sep":                     s = fit.samples[int(r.integers(len(fit.samples)))]                     a.update({"orbital.sep_events_per_year": float(s[0]), "orbital.sep_mean_duration_s": float(s[1]),                               "orbital.sep_intensity_median": float(s[2]), "orbital.sep_intensity_log_sigma": float(s[3])})                 elif key == "power":                     s = fit.samples[int(r.integers(len(fit.samples)))]                     a.update({"power.base_power_w": float(s[0]), "power.static_frac": float(s[1]),                               "power.leak_temp_coeff": float(s[2]), "power.clock_frac": float(s[3])})                 elif key == "thermal":                     s = fit.samples[int(r.integers(len(fit.samples)))]                     vals, _ = thermal_to_config_values(cfg, *s)                     for k, v in vals.items():                         if not (k == "earth_view_fraction" and "Q_ecl" in fit.fixed) and not (k == "sun_projected_fraction" and "Q_sun" in fit.fixed):                             a[f"thermal.{k}"] = float(v)                 elif key == "perf":                     s = fit.samples[int(r.integers(len(fit.samples)))]                     for n, v in zip(("compute.pipeline_overlap", "memory.hbm_efficiency", "compute.sparse_speedup_at_50"), s):                         a[n] = float(v)             draws.append(a)         return {"n_draws": len(draws), "sources": sorted(fits)}, []     step("epistemic draws", _draws, bool(fits))     report_cfg = cfg   # best-estimate config     def _flight():         nonlocal weights         wts = np.ones(len(draws))         cfgs = []         for a in draws:             c = report_cfg.copy()             for path, val in a.items():                 set_by_path(c, path, CrossSection(**vars(val)) if isinstance(val, CrossSection) else val)             cfgs.append(c)         warns: List[str] = []         for log in data.flight:             E = np.array([expected_events_for_flight(c, log.region, log.n_units, log.segments, log.flare_events) for c in cfgs])             fu = flight_update(E, log.events, opts.flight_discrepancy_log_sigma, log=log.name)             flight_updates.append(fu); warns += [f"{log.name}: {w}" for w in fu.warnings]             wts = wts * fu.weights         weights = wts / wts.sum()         return {"logs": [f.log for f in flight_updates],                 "combined_ess": float(1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2))}, warns     step("flight-data update", _flight, bool(data.flight) and bool(draws))     # rate summary (SRAM, plus any region with a fitted ion cross-section)     if draws:         regions = sorted({k.split(":")[1] for k in fits if k.startswith("xs:")})         for region in regions:             vals = np.array([_static_rate_per_bit_day(                 _apply(report_cfg, a), region) for a in draws])             lo, med, hi = weighted_quantile(vals, np.ones(len(vals)), [0.025, 0.5, 0.975])             d = {"placeholder": _static_rate_per_bit_day(_with_env(placeholder_cfg, cfg), region),                  "median": float(med), "lo": float(lo), "hi": float(hi)}             if flight_updates:                 lo2, med2, hi2 = weighted_quantile(vals, weights, [0.025, 0.5, 0.975])                 d["posterior"] = {"median": float(med2), "lo": float(lo2), "hi": float(hi2)}             d.update(_placeholder_dependence(report_cfg, region))             rate_summary[region] = d     audit = placeholder_audit(cfg)     return CalibrationReport(cfg, initial_hash, steps, audit, opts, fits, draws, weights, flight_updates, rate_summary,                              provenance(cfg, opts.seed)) def _placeholder_dependence(cfg: SimConfig, region: str) -> dict:     """What share of this region's orbit-static rate is carried by cross-section families that are STILL placeholders?     The reported interval does not include their uncertainty, so a high share means the interval is not trustworthy."""     orbit = OrbitalEnvironment(cfg.orbital)     orbit.flares = FlareSchedule.from_events([])     env = RadiationEnvironment(cfg, orbit)     sc, comp = env.scalars, env.base[region]     part = {"ion": comp["gcr"] * sc["geo"] * sc["solar_gcr"], "proton": comp["trapped"] * sc["trapped"] * sc["proton_shield"]}     unresolved = [k for k in ("ion", "proton")                   if getattr(getattr(cfg.radiation, region), k).sigma_sat_cm2 > 0 and                   any(f"radiation.{region}.{k}.{p}" not in cfg.calibration for p in ("sigma_sat_cm2", "x_th", "width", "shape"))]     total = sum(part.values())     frac = sum(part[k] for k in unresolved) / total if total > 0 else 0.0     return {"unresolved_kinds": unresolved, "unresolved_fraction": float(frac)} def _apply(cfg: SimConfig, assign: Dict[str, object]) -> SimConfig:     c = cfg.copy()     for path, val in assign.items():         set_by_path(c, path, CrossSection(**vars(val)) if isinstance(val, CrossSection) else val)     return c def _with_env(placeholder_cfg: SimConfig, calibrated: SimConfig) -> SimConfig:     """Placeholder cross-sections evaluated in the calibrated environment (isolates the effect of the beam data)."""     c = placeholder_cfg.copy()     c.radiation.environment_mode = calibrated.radiation.environment_mode     c.radiation.tables = {k: type(v)(list(v.x), list(v.phi_integral)) for k, v in calibrated.radiation.tables.items()}     return c # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- def epistemic_mission_study(report: CalibrationReport, layers, mission_s: float, n: int = 16, duty: float = 1.0,                             dt_max_s: float = 30.0, seed: int = 0) -> Dict[str, np.ndarray]:     """Push epistemic parameter draws through the simulator: spread of mission outputs due to calibration uncertainty."""     from ..simulator import run_mission     cfgs = report.sample_configs(n, make_rng(seed, "epistemic"))     out = {k: [] for k in ("T_max_k", "avg_tflops", "avg_power_w", "sdc_per_hr", "seu_sram")}     for i, c in enumerate(cfgs):         r = run_mission(c, layers, mission_s, duty, dt_max_s, seed=child_seed(seed, "epi", i))         out["T_max_k"].append(r.T_max_k); out["avg_tflops"].append(r.avg_tflops_dense)         out["avg_power_w"].append(r.avg_power_w); out["sdc_per_hr"].append(r.radiation.sdc_per_hr)         out["seu_sram"].append(r.radiation.n_seu_sram)     return {k: np.array(v) for k, v in out.items()} # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- # Plan files / CLI # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- def load_plan(path) -> Tuple[SimConfig, CalibrationData, CalibrationOptions]:     path = Path(path)     plan = json.loads(path.read_text())     base = path.parent     cfg = SimConfig.from_json((base / plan["base_config"]).read_text()) if plan.get("base_config") else SimConfig()     data = CalibrationData()     env = plan.get("environment")     if env:         data.env_tables = {k: D.load_spectrum_csv(base / f) for k, f in env["tables"].items()}         data.env_source = env.get("source", "environment tables")     for b in plan.get("beam", []):         data.beam.append(D.load_beam_csv(base / b["file"], b["region"], b["kind"], b.get("name")))     if "mbu" in plan:         data.mbu = D.load_mbu_csv(base / plan["mbu"]["file"])     if "sep" in plan:         data.sep = D.load_sep_csv(base / plan["sep"]["file"], plan["sep"]["obs_span_s"])     if "power" in plan:         data.power = D.load_power_csv(base / plan["power"]["file"]); data.power_source = plan["power"].get("source", "power measurements")     if "thermal" in plan:         data.thermal = D.load_thermal_csv(base / plan["thermal"]["file"]); data.thermal_source = plan["thermal"].get("source", "thermal telemetry")     if "perf" in plan:         data.perf = D.load_perf_csv(base / plan["perf"]["file"]); data.perf_source = plan["perf"].get("source", "performance microbenchmarks")     for f in plan.get("flight", []):         data.flight.append(D.FlightLog(f["name"], f["region"], f["n_units"], tuple(map(tuple, f["segments"])), int(f["events"]),                                        tuple(map(tuple, f.get("flare_events", [])))))     for a in plan.get("avf", []):         data.avf.append(D.AvfRequest(**{**a, "shape": tuple(a.get("shape", (32, 32, 32)))}))     for d in plan.get("design_facts", []):         data.design_facts.append(D.DesignFact(d["path"], d["value"], d["source"]))     return cfg, data, CalibrationOptions(**plan.get("options", {})) def main(argv: Optional[Sequence[str]] = None) -> int:     ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="python -m uchu.calibration", description="Run the Uchu Gijutsu calibration workflow.")     ap.add_argument("plan", help="calibration plan (JSON)")     ap.add_argument("--out", default="calibration_out", help="output directory")     args = ap.parse_args(argv)     t0 = time.time()     try:         cfg, data, opts = load_plan(args.plan)     except (CalibrationDataError, OSError, KeyError, ValueError) as e:         print(f"PLAN ERROR: {e}", file=sys.stderr)         return 3     rep = run_calibration(cfg, data, opts)     out = rep.save(args.out)     print(rep.audit.render())     for s in rep.steps:         print(f"  [{s.status:7s}] {s.name}" + (f"  -- {s.error}" if s.error else ""))     print(f"wrote {out}/ in {time.time() - t0:.1f}s")     return 2 if any(s.status == "failed" for s in rep.steps) else 0 if __name__ == "__main__":     sys.exit(main()) ``` ### `uchu/calibration/synthetic.py` ```python """Synthetic measurement campaigns generated from a hidden TRUTH configuration. Purpose: prove the workflow can recover known parameters (and quantify when it cannot) before it is pointed at real data. Synthetic data are generated by the SAME models the calibration fits, so a pass demonstrates the fitting machinery (estimator, uncertainty, identifiability handling) -- it does not demonstrate that the models describe real hardware. """ from __future__ import annotations import json import math from pathlib import Path from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Sequence, Tuple import numpy as np from ..config import CrossSection, DType, SimConfig, SpectrumTable from ..memory import WorkloadLayer, estimate_layer from ..orbit import FlareSchedule, OrbitalEnvironment from ..radiation import RadiationEnvironment, TruncatedPowerLaw from ..thermal import LumpedThermalModel from .data import (BeamDataset, BeamPoint, FlightLog, MbuDataset, PerfMeasurement, PowerMeasurement, SepCatalog,                    ThermalSample) from .fit_hardware import suggest_gemm_shapes, suggest_power_design from .fit_radiation import _cluster_pmf, expected_events_for_flight def bake_environment_tables(cfg: SimConfig, n: int = 1500) -> SimConfig:     """Convert a 'scaled_placeholder' config into an equivalent 'tabulated_orbit' config (placeholder scalings baked into     the tables).  Also the migration path for anyone holding v3.0 power-law configs."""     c = cfg.copy()     rc = c.radiation     env = RadiationEnvironment(c, OrbitalEnvironment(c.orbital))     sc = env.scalars     plan = {"gcr_ion": (rc.gcr_ion, rc.let_grid_lo, sc["geo"] * sc["solar_gcr"]),             "trapped_proton": (rc.trapped_proton, rc.energy_grid_lo, sc["trapped"] * sc["proton_shield"]),             "sep_ion": (rc.sep_ion, rc.let_grid_lo, sc["geo"]),             "sep_proton": (rc.sep_proton, rc.energy_grid_lo, sc["proton_shield"] * sc["geo"])}     tables: Dict[str, SpectrumTable] = {}     for key, (sp, lo, scale) in plan.items():         if scale <= 0:             continue         law = TruncatedPowerLaw(sp.phi_ref, sp.x_ref, sp.gamma, sp.x_max)         x = np.geomspace(lo, sp.x_max, n)         tables[key] = SpectrumTable(x.tolist(), (law.integral(x) * scale).tolist())     rc.tables = tables     rc.environment_mode = "tabulated_orbit"     return c.validate() def make_truth_config() -> SimConfig:     """A deliberately non-default 'real device': every placeholder family differs from the shipped default."""     c = bake_environment_tables(SimConfig())     r = c.radiation     r.sram.ion = CrossSection(3.2e-14, 1.2, 14.0, 1.7)     r.sram.proton = CrossSection(1.5e-14, 20.0, 35.0, 1.5)       # ~3x the placeholder proton sensitivity     r.register.ion = CrossSection(5.5e-14, 0.9, 10.0, 1.5)     r.mbu_prob, r.mbu_cluster_geom_p, r.mbu_max_cluster = 0.18, 0.45, 5     c.orbital.sep_events_per_year, c.orbital.sep_mean_duration_s = 4.2, 30 * 3600.0     c.orbital.sep_intensity_median, c.orbital.sep_intensity_log_sigma = 0.8, 1.3     c.power.base_power_w, c.power.static_frac, c.power.leak_temp_coeff, c.power.clock_frac = 63.0, 0.38, 3.1, 0.12     c.thermal.capacitance_j_per_k, c.thermal.radiator_area_m2 = 320.0, 0.21     c.thermal.earth_view_fraction, c.thermal.sun_projected_fraction = 0.32, 0.22     c.memory.hbm_bw_tb_s_per_stack = 0.15           # datasheet value (known to the analyst too)     c.memory.hbm_efficiency, c.compute.pipeline_overlap, c.compute.sparse_speedup_at_50 = 0.72, 0.6, 1.7     return c.validate() def analyst_start_config(truth: SimConfig) -> SimConfig:     """What the analyst has before calibrating: datasheet facts and the orbit, but default placeholders everywhere else."""     c = SimConfig()     c.orbital = truth.orbital.__class__(**{**vars(truth.orbital)})     c.orbital.sep_events_per_year = None     c.orbital.sep_mean_duration_s, c.orbital.sep_intensity_median, c.orbital.sep_intensity_log_sigma = 12 * 3600.0, 1.0, 1.0     c.memory.hbm_bw_tb_s_per_stack = truth.memory.hbm_bw_tb_s_per_stack     return c.validate() # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- def gen_beam(truth: CrossSection, region: str, kind: str, name: str, rng: np.random.Generator, n_units: float,              x_grid: Sequence[float], target_events: float = 150.0, f_min: float = 1e5, f_max: float = 1e8) -> BeamDataset:     """Beam runs the way they are done: run each point to ~target_events or until fluence f_max."""     pts = []     for x in x_grid:         sig = float(truth(x))         F = f_max if sig <= 0 else float(np.clip(target_events / (sig * n_units), f_min, f_max))         pts.append(BeamPoint(float(x), F, int(rng.poisson(sig * n_units * F)), float(n_units)))     return BeamDataset(name, region, kind, tuple(pts)) def gen_mbu(truth: SimConfig, n_strikes: int, rng: np.random.Generator, name: str = "mbu") -> MbuDataset:     r = truth.radiation     pmf = np.concatenate([[1 - r.mbu_prob], r.mbu_prob * _cluster_pmf(r.mbu_cluster_geom_p, r.mbu_max_cluster)])     sizes = rng.choice(np.arange(1, r.mbu_max_cluster + 1), size=n_strikes, p=pmf)     return MbuDataset(name, {int(m): int((sizes == m).sum()) for m in np.unique(sizes)}) def gen_sep_catalog(truth: SimConfig, years: float, rng: np.random.Generator, name: str = "sep") -> SepCatalog:     o = truth.orbital     n = int(rng.poisson(o.sep_events_per_year * years))     return SepCatalog(name, years * 365.25 * 86400.0, tuple(rng.exponential(o.sep_mean_duration_s, n)),                       tuple(rng.lognormal(math.log(o.sep_intensity_median), o.sep_intensity_log_sigma, n))) def gen_power(truth: SimConfig, rng: np.random.Generator, rel_noise: float = 0.01) -> List[PowerMeasurement]:     from ..thermal import PowerModel as PM     pm, out = PM(truth), []     for d in suggest_power_design():         f, v = truth.dvfs.nom_freq_ghz * d["f_frac"], truth.dvfs.nom_voltage_v * d["v_frac"]         out.append(PowerMeasurement(d["T_k"], f, v, d["util"], pm.power_w(d["T_k"], f, v, d["util"]) * (1 + rng.normal(0, rel_noise))))     return out def gen_thermal(truth: SimConfig, rng: np.random.Generator, n_orbits: int = 3, dt: float = 30.0, noise_K: float = 0.3,                 T0: float = 285.0) -> List[ThermalSample]:     orb, th = OrbitalEnvironment(truth.orbital), LumpedThermalModel(truth)     th.T = T0     rows = [ThermalSample(0.0, 0.0, False, T0)]     for i in range(1, int(n_orbits * orb.period_s / dt) + 1):         t1 = i * dt         P = 35.0 + 25.0 * (0.5 + 0.5 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * t1 / 1900.0))         ecl = orb.in_eclipse(t1)         th.step(dt, lambda T, P=P: P, ecl)         rows.append(ThermalSample(t1, P, ecl, th.T + rng.normal(0, noise_K)))     return rows def gen_perf(truth: SimConfig, start: SimConfig, rng: np.random.Generator, t_launch: float = 8e-6, log_noise: float = 0.02,              freqs: Sequence[float] = (1.0, 1.5), shapes: Optional[Sequence[dict]] = None) -> List[PerfMeasurement]:     dt = {d.label: d for d in DType}     out = []     for s in (shapes if shapes is not None else suggest_gemm_shapes(start)):         for f in freqs:             L = WorkloadLayer("m", "g", s["M"], s["K"], s["N"], dt[s["dtype"]], s["sparsity"])             lat = (estimate_layer(L, truth, f).duration_s + t_launch) * math.exp(rng.normal(0, log_noise))             out.append(PerfMeasurement(s["M"], s["K"], s["N"], s["dtype"], s["sparsity"], f, lat))     return out def gen_flight(truth: SimConfig, region: str, n_units: float, years: float, rng: np.random.Generator,                name: str = "flight", gap_fraction: float = 0.1) -> FlightLog:     """On-orbit EDAC log: monitored for `years` with a few outage gaps; flares from a (known) SEP catalogue."""     span = years * 365.25 * 86400.0     sched = FlareSchedule(truth.orbital.sep_events_per_year / (365.25 * 86400.0), rng,                           truth.orbital.sep_mean_duration_s, truth.orbital.sep_intensity_log_sigma,                           truth.orbital.sep_intensity_median)     sched.extend_to(span)     flares = tuple(e for e in sched.events if e[0] < span)     cut = rng.uniform(0.3, 0.7) * span     gap = gap_fraction * span     segs = ((0.0, cut), (cut + gap, span))     E = expected_events_for_flight(truth, region, n_units, segs, flares)     return FlightLog(name, region, n_units, segs, int(rng.poisson(E)), flares) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- def write_campaign(out_dir, truth: SimConfig, start: SimConfig, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Path:     """Write a complete on-disk campaign (CSV files + plan.json) so the CLI path is exercised end to end."""     out = Path(out_dir); out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)     import csv     def w(name, header, rows):         with open(out / name, "w", newline="") as fh:             cw = csv.writer(fh); cw.writerow(header); cw.writerows(rows)     for k, t in truth.radiation.tables.items():         w(f"env_{k}.csv", ["x", "phi_integral"], zip(t.x, t.phi_integral))     nb = 1e8     plan: dict = {"base_config": "start_config.json", "options": {"seed": 7, "n_boot": 80, "n_draws": 80},                   "environment": {"source": "synthetic CREME-like tables", "tables": {k: f"env_{k}.csv" for k in truth.radiation.tables}},                   "beam": [], "design_facts": [{"path": "radiation.gates_per_pe", "value": 1800, "source": "synthetic netlist"}]}     ion_x = [1.5, 2.2, 3.3, 5.0, 7.5,
