11.0, 17.0, 26.0, 40.0, 60.0, 85.0]     pro_x = [12.0, 18.0, 25.0, 35.0, 50.0, 70.0, 100.0, 150.0, 200.0]     for region, kind in (("sram", "ion"), ("sram", "proton"), ("register", "ion")):         xs = getattr(getattr(truth.radiation, region), kind)         ds = gen_beam(xs, region, kind, f"{region}_{kind}", rng, nb if region == "sram" else 1.2e7, ion_x if kind == "ion" else pro_x)         col = "let_mev_cm2_mg" if kind == "ion" else "energy_mev"         w(f"beam_{region}_{kind}.csv", [col, "fluence_cm2", "events", "n_units"],           [(p.x, p.fluence_cm2, p.events, p.n_units) for p in ds.points])         plan["beam"].append({"file": f"beam_{region}_{kind}.csv", "region": region, "kind": kind, "name": f"{region}_{kind}"})     m = gen_mbu(truth, 600, rng)     w("mbu.csv", ["cluster_size", "count"], sorted(m.counts.items())); plan["mbu"] = {"file": "mbu.csv"}     s = gen_sep_catalog(truth, 25.0, rng)     w("sep.csv", ["duration_s", "intensity_rel"], zip(s.durations_s, s.intensities_rel))     plan["sep"] = {"file": "sep.csv", "obs_span_s": s.obs_span_s}     w("power.csv", ["T_k", "f_ghz", "v_v", "util", "power_w"], [(p.T_k, p.f_ghz, p.v_v, p.util, p.power_w) for p in gen_power(truth, rng)])     plan["power"] = {"file": "power.csv", "source": "synthetic bench sweep"}     w("thermal.csv", ["t_s", "power_w", "eclipse", "T_k"], [(r.t_s, r.power_w, int(r.eclipse), r.T_k) for r in gen_thermal(truth, rng)])     plan["thermal"] = {"file": "thermal.csv", "source": "synthetic flight-like telemetry"}     w("perf.csv", ["M", "K", "N", "dtype", "sparsity", "freq_ghz", "latency_s"],       [(p.M, p.K, p.N, p.dtype, p.sparsity, p.freq_ghz, p.latency_s) for p in gen_perf(truth, start, rng)])     plan["perf"] = {"file": "perf.csv", "source": "synthetic microbenchmarks"}     fl = gen_flight(truth, "sram", 5.4e8, 2.0, rng)     plan["flight"] = [{"name": fl.name, "region": fl.region, "n_units": fl.n_units, "segments": [list(x) for x in fl.segments],                        "events": fl.events, "flare_events": [list(x) for x in fl.flare_events]}]     plan["avf"] = [{"field": "avf_sram", "dtype": "int8", "tol": 0.01, "trials_per_bit": 40}]     (out / "start_config.json").write_text(start.to_json())     (out / "plan.json").write_text(json.dumps(plan, indent=2))     return out / "plan.json" ``` --- ## 9. Tests ### `tests/__init__.py` ```python """Test package (enables `from tests._campaign import ...`).""" ``` ### `tests/_campaign.py` ```python """Shared builder for synthetic calibration campaigns (hidden truth -> measurements).""" from uchu import make_rng from uchu.calibration import CalibrationData from uchu.calibration.data import AvfRequest, DesignFact from uchu.calibration.synthetic import (gen_beam, gen_flight, gen_mbu, gen_perf, gen_power, gen_sep_catalog, gen_thermal,                                         analyst_start_config, make_truth_config) ION_X = [1.5, 2.2, 3.3, 5.0, 7.5, 11.0, 17.0, 26.0, 40.0, 60.0, 85.0] PRO_X = [12., 18., 25., 35., 50., 70., 100., 150., 200.] TRUTH = make_truth_config() START = analyst_start_config(TRUTH) def build(seed, truth=TRUTH, start=START, *, flight=True, hardware=True, mbu=True, sep=True, register=True, avf=True,           n_mbu=600, sep_years=25.0, flight_years=2.0):     rng = make_rng(seed, "e2e")     d = CalibrationData(env_tables=truth.radiation.tables, env_source="synthetic CREME tables")     d.beam = [gen_beam(truth.radiation.sram.ion, "sram", "ion", "sram_ion", rng, 1e8, ION_X),               gen_beam(truth.radiation.sram.proton, "sram", "proton", "sram_proton", rng, 1e8, PRO_X)]     if register:         d.beam.append(gen_beam(truth.radiation.register.ion, "register", "ion", "reg_ion", rng, 1.2e7, ION_X))     if mbu:         d.mbu = gen_mbu(truth, n_mbu, rng)     if sep:         d.sep = gen_sep_catalog(truth, sep_years, rng)     if hardware:         d.power, d.thermal, d.perf = gen_power(truth, rng), gen_thermal(truth, rng), gen_perf(truth, start, rng)     if flight:         d.flight = [gen_flight(truth, "sram", 5.4e8, flight_years, rng)]     if avf:         d.avf = [AvfRequest("avf_sram", "int8", 1e-2, 40)]     d.design_facts = [DesignFact("radiation.gates_per_pe", 1800, "synthetic netlist")]     return d ``` ### `tests/test_rng_config.py` ```python import numpy as np import pytest from uchu import SimConfig, make_rng, child_seed, provenance from uchu.config import DType, ProcessNode from uchu.stats import poisson_rate_ci, wilson_ci def test_same_keys_same_stream_different_keys_differ():     a = make_rng(1, "x").random(5)     assert np.array_equal(a, make_rng(1, "x").random(5))     assert not np.array_equal(a, make_rng(1, "y").random(5))     assert not np.array_equal(a, make_rng(2, "x").random(5)) def test_child_seed_deterministic_and_distinct():     assert child_seed(7, "s", 1) == child_seed(7, "s", 1)     assert len({child_seed(7, "s", i) for i in range(1000)}) == 1000 def test_global_rng_untouched():     np.random.seed(123)     before = np.random.get_state()[1].copy()     make_rng(5, "a").random(100)     assert np.array_equal(before, np.random.get_state()[1]) def test_default_config_validates_and_hash_is_stable():     c = SimConfig().validate()     assert c.config_hash() == SimConfig().config_hash()     c2 = c.copy(); c2.compute.array_size = 128     assert c2.config_hash() != c.config_hash() @pytest.mark.parametrize("mutate", [     lambda c: setattr(c.compute, "array_size", 0),     lambda c: setattr(c.power, "static_frac", 1.0),     lambda c: setattr(c.dvfs, "nom_voltage_v", 2.0),     lambda c: setattr(c.radiation, "mbu_prob", 1.5),     lambda c: setattr(c.radiation, "scrub_interval_s", 0.0),     lambda c: setattr(c.thermal, "capacitance_j_per_k", -1.0), ]) def test_invalid_configs_rejected(mutate):     c = SimConfig(); mutate(c)     with pytest.raises(ValueError):         c.validate() def test_dtype_throughput_is_not_inverted():     assert DType.INT4.pe_throughput > DType.INT8.pe_throughput > DType.FP16.pe_throughput > DType.FP32.pe_throughput def test_provenance_fields():     p = provenance(SimConfig())     assert {"model_version", "config_hash", "seed", "numpy", "scipy"} <= set(p) def test_poisson_ci_covers_and_zero_case():     lo, hi = test_poisson_ci_coverage_monte_carlo():     rng = make_rng(0, "cov")     lam, n, cover = 7.3, 4000, 0     for k in rng.poisson(lam, size=n):         lo, hi = poisson_rate_ci(int(k), 1.0)         cover += lo <= lam <= hi     assert cover / n >= 0.94          # exact interval is conservative (>= nominal 95%) def test_wilson_ci_basic():     lo, hi = wilson_ci(50, 100)     assert 0.40 < lo < 0.5 < hi < 0.60 def test_bootstrap_ci_brackets_mean_and_shrinks_with_n():     from uchu.stats import bootstrap_ci     rng = make_rng(0, "b")     small, large = rng.normal(5.0, 2.0, 30), rng.normal(5.0, 2.0, 3000)     lo, hi = bootstrap_ci(small, rng=make_rng(1, "bb"))     assert lo < small.mean() < hi     lo2, hi2 = bootstrap_ci(large, n_boot=500, rng=make_rng(1, "bb"))     assert (hi2 - lo2) < 0.3 * (hi - lo) and lo2 < 5.0 < hi2 ``` ### `tests/test_memory.py` ```python import math import pytest from uchu import DType, SimConfig, WorkloadLayer, estimate_layer, structured_sparsity_speedup from uchu.memory import hbm_bandwidth_bytes_s, profile_pass, weight_bytes_factor F = 1.5 def cfg(**kw):     c = SimConfig()     for k, v in kw.items():         setattr(c.compute if k in ("array_size", "pipeline_overlap") else c.memory, k, v)     return c.validate() def test_cycle_count_exact_single_tile():     c = cfg(array_size=64)     l = WorkloadLayer("t", "linear", 64, 128, 64, DType.INT8)     p = estimate_layer(l, c, F)     assert p.t_compute_s == pytest.approx((128 + 2 * 64 - 2) / (F * 1e9)) def test_tiling_quantisation_penalty():     c = cfg(array_size=64)     a = estimate_layer(WorkloadLayer("a", "linear", 64, 4096, 64), c, F)     b = estimate_layer(WorkloadLayer("b", "linear", 65, 4096, 64), c, F)   # 2 row-tiles for 1 extra row     assert b.t_compute_s == pytest.approx(2 * a.t_compute_s)     assert b.util < 0.55 * a.util + 1e-9 or b.util < a.util * 0.6 def test_peak_matches_closed_form():     c = cfg()     p = estimate_layer(WorkloadLayer("t", "linear", 4096, 4096, 4096), c, F)     assert p.peak_exec_tflops == pytest.approx(2 * 256 * 256 * F * 1e9 / 1e12) def test_large_gemm_is_compute_bound_and_near_peak():     c = cfg()     p = estimate_layer(WorkloadLayer("t", "linear", 8192, 8192, 8192), c, F)     assert not p.memory_bound     assert p.util > 0.9 def test_gemv_on_output_stationary_array_is_compute_limited_by_idle_rows():     """M=1 uses 1 of 256 PE rows: the array, not HBM, is the bottleneck at default bandwidth."""     p = estimate_layer(WorkloadLayer("gemv", "linear", 1, 8192, 8192), cfg(), F)     assert not p.memory_bound and p.util < 0.01 def test_memory_bound_when_bandwidth_is_scarce():     slow = cfg(hbm_bw_tb_s_per_stack=0.05)     p = estimate_layer(WorkloadLayer("gemv", "linear", 1, 8192, 8192), slow, F)     assert p.memory_bound and p.t_memory_s > p.t_compute_s     assert p.duration_s == pytest.approx(p.t_memory_s) def test_roofline_crossover_follows_arithmetic_intensity():     """With full array utilisation (aligned dims, large K) memory_bound <=> AI < ridge."""     slow = cfg(hbm_bw_tb_s_per_stack=0.05)     lo = estimate_layer(WorkloadLayer("lo", "linear", 256, 4096, 4096), slow, F) hi.ridge_point and not hi.memory_bound def test_duration_is_max_of_the_two_roofs_everywhere():     c = cfg(hbm_bw_tb_s_per_stack=0.2)     for M in (1, 7, 64, 256, 300, 4096):         for K in (64, 1000, 4096):             for N in (64, 513, 4096):                 p = estimate_layer(WorkloadLayer("r", "linear", M, K, N), c, F)                 assert p.duration_s == pytest.approx(max(p.t_compute_s, p.t_memory_s))                 assert p.memory_bound == (p.t_memory_s > p.t_compute_s)                 assert p.executed_flops / p.duration_s <= p.peak_exec_tflops * 1e12 * (1 + 1e-12) def test_sparsity_counted_once_never_exceeds_nominal_speedup():     c = cfg()     d = estimate_layer(WorkloadLayer("d", "linear", 8192, 8192, 8192, DType.INT8, 0.0), c, F)     s = estimate_layer(WorkloadLayer("s", "linear", 8192, 8192, 8192, DType.INT8, 0.5), c, F)     assert s.executed_flops == pytest.approx(d.executed_flops / 1.85)     assert 1.7 < d.duration_s / s.duration_s < 1.85       # fill/drain does not shrink -> < 1.85, never 1.85^2     assert s.dense_flops == d.dense_flops def test_sparsity_speedup_approaches_nominal_for_deep_reductions():     c = cfg()     d = estimate_layer(WorkloadLayer("d", "linear", 256, 131072, 256, DType.INT8, 0.0), c, F)     s = estimate_layer(WorkloadLayer("s", "linear", 256, 131072, 256, DType.INT8, 0.5), c, F)     assert d.duration_s / s.duration_s == pytest.approx(1.85, rel=0.01) def test_pipeline_overlap_hides_fill_drain():     l = WorkloadLayer("a", "linear", 1024, 64, 1024)      # 16 tiles, shallow K: fill/drain dominates     exposed = estimate_layer(l, cfg(array_size=64, pipeline_overlap=0.0), F)     hidden = estimate_layer(l, cfg(array_size=64, pipeline_overlap=1.0), F)     tiles = 16 * 16     assert exposed.t_compute_s == pytest.approx(tiles * (64 + 126) / (F * 1e9))     assert hidden.t_compute_s == pytest.approx((tiles * 64 + 126) / (F * 1e9))     assert hidden.util / exposed.util == pytest.approx((tiles * 190) / (tiles * 64 + 126), rel=1e-9) def test_dtype_scaling_int8_vs_fp16():     c = cfg()     i8 = estimate_layer(WorkloadLayer("a", "linear", 256, 131072, 256, DType.INT8), c, 3 * 1.0     assert big.hbm_bytes == pytest.approx(compulsory)     assert small.hbm_bytes > 2 * big.hbm_bytes def test_ecc_overhead_reduces_bandwidth():     on = cfg()     from uchu import build_transformer_workload     L = build_transformer_workload(num_layers=2)     pr = profile_pass(L, c, F)     assert 0.0 < pr.util <= 1.0     assert pr.duration_s == `tests/test_thermal.py` ```python import math import numpy as np import pytest from uchu import LumpedThermalModel, PowerModel, SimConfig from uchu.config import ProcessNode from uchu.thermal import def test_dynamic_power_scales_with_f_and_v_squared():     pm = PowerModel(SimConfig())     a = pm.breakdown(300, 1.5, 0.9)     b = pm.breakdown(300, 0.75, 0.9 * 0.5)     for k in ("clock", "logic", >= 0.1 * 1.5 - 1e-12 def test_steady_state_matches_closed_form_for_constant_power():     c = SimConfig()     th = LumpedThermalModel(c)     P = 80.0     T = th.steady_state(lambda T: P, eclipse=True)     k = c.thermal.emissivity * SIGMA_SB * c.thermal.view_factor * c.thermal.radiator_area_m2     assert T == pytest.approx(((P + th.env_load_w(True)) / k) ** 0.25, rel=1e-9) def test_time_integration_converges_to_steady_state():     c = SimConfig()     th = LumpedThermalModel(c)     th.T = 200.0     T_ss = th.steady_state(lambda T: 60.0, True)     for _ in range(2000):                      # 60 000 s ~ 90 thermal time constants         th.step(30.0, lambda T: 60.0, True)     assert th.T == pytest.approx(T_ss, rel=1e-9)   # the BE fixed point IS the steady state def _integrate(dt, t_end=300.0, T0=200.0):     th = LumpedThermalModel(SimConfig()); th.T = T0     for _ in range(int(round(t_end / dt))):         th.step(dt, lambda T: 70.0, False)     return th.T def test_first_order_convergence_in_dt():     ref = _integrate(0.05)     e = [abs(_integrate(dt) - ref) for dt in (60.0, 30.0, 15.0)]     assert e[0] > e[1] > e[2]     assert 1.5 < e[0] / e[1] < 2.6 and 1.5 < e[1] / e[2] < 2.6     # backward Euler: O(dt) def test_unconditionally_stable_with_huge_step():     th = LumpedThermalModel(SimConfig()); th.T = 200.0     T_ss = th.steady_state(lambda T: 60.0, False)     th.step(1e7, lambda T: 60.0, False)     # one finite BE step leaves a residual ~ C (T-T0) / (dt h') ~ 5e-3 K; it must be finite and tiny     assert math.isfinite(th.T) and abs(th.T - T_ss) < 0.02 def test_energy_balance_closes_per_step():     c = SimConfig(); th = LumpedThermalModel(c); th.T = 250.0     pm = PowerModel(c)     pf = lambda T: pm.power_w(T, 1.5, 0.9, 1.0)     for dt in (0.01, 1.0, 20.0, 400.0):         T0 = th.T         T1 = th.step(dt, pf, False)         stored = c.thermal.capacitance_j_per_k * (T1 - T0)         net = (pf(T1) + th.env_load_w(False) - th.radiated_w(T1)) * dt      # implicit-Euler end-point rule         assert stored == pytest.approx(net, rel=1e-7, abs=1e-7) def test_sunlit_hotter_than_eclipse():     c = SimConfig(); th = LumpedThermalModel(c)     pf = lambda T: 60.0     assert th.steady_state(pf, False) > th.steady_state(pf, True) def test_cooling_direction_and_bounds():     th = LumpedThermalModel(SimConfig()); th.T = 600.0     th.step(100.0, lambda T: 10.0, True)     assert 3.0 < th.T < 600.0 def test_thermal_runaway_detected():     c = SimConfig()     c.power.leak_temp_coeff = 12.0     c.thermal.radiator_area_m2 = 0.02     pm = PowerModel(c); th = LumpedThermalModel(c)     th.T = 380.0     for _ in range(50):         th.step(60.0, lambda T: pm.power_w(T, 1.5, 0.9, 1.0), False)         if th.runaway:             break     assert th.runaway     assert pm  # keep linter quiet def test_view_factor_degrades_with_mission_year():     c = 1.0), False)     assert T is not None and T < c.dvfs.throttle_onset_k ``` ### `tests/test_orbit.py` ```python import math import numpy as np import pytest from uchu import SimConfig, make_rng from def test_kepler_periods():     assert kepler_period_s(400) == pytest.approx(5554, rel=2e-3)     assert kepler_period_s(35786) == pytest.approx(86164, rel=2e-3)     # sidereal day def o.eclipse_segments(0, 1e6) == [(0, 1e6, False)] @pytest.mark.parametrize("t0,t1", [(0.0, 1.0), (100.0, 20000.0), (5000.3, 5001.7), (0.0, 100000.0)]) def test_eclipse_segments_partition_interval_exactly(t0, t1):     o = OrbitalEnvironment(OrbitalConfig())     segs = o.eclipse_segments(t0, t1)     assert segs[0][0] == t0 and segs[-1][1] == t1     for - a for a, b, e in segs if e)     assert total_ecl == pytest.approx(o.eclipse_fraction * (t1 - t0), abs=o.period_s * o.eclipse_fraction) def test_eclipse_time_over_many_orbits_matches_fraction(): o.eclipse_fraction, rel=1e-9) def test_flare_integral_exact_and_additive():     s = FlareSchedule.from_events([(100.0, 200.0, 2.0), (150.0, 400.0, 1.0)])     assert s.intensity_integral(0, 1000) == pytest.approx(s.intensity_integral(0, 1000))     assert s.intensity_at(160) == 3.0 and s.intensity_at(250) == 1.0 and s.intensity_at(500) == 0.0 def test_flare_integral_matches_brute_force():     s = FlareSchedule.from_events([(10.0, 55.5, 1.7), (40.0, 90.0, 0.4)])     t = np.linspace(0, 100, 200001)     brute = sum(s.intensity_at(x) for x in t[::200]) * (t[200] - t[0])     assert s.intensity_integral(0, 100) == pytest.approx(brute, rel=0.02) def test_schedule_prefix_independent_of_query_order():     a = FlareSchedule(1 / 1e5, make_rng(3, "f")); b = FlareSchedule(1 / 1e5, == b.events[:n] def test_flare_arrival_rate_statistics():     rate = SEP_EVENTS_PER_YEAR[SolarActivity.HIGH] / (365.25 * 86400)     counts = []     for i in range(200):         s = FlareSchedule(rate, import pytest from scipy.integrate import quad from uchu import (EccMemory, RadiationEnvironment, RadiationSubsystem, SimConfig, TruncatedPowerLaw,                   integral_rate, make_rng) from uchu.config import CrossSection, OrbitalConfig, OrbitType, SolarActivity from uchu.orbit import FlareSchedule, OrbitalEnvironment from uchu.radiation import RadiationStats, TabulatedSpectrum LAW = TruncatedPowerLaw(2e-3, 1.0, 1.6, 150.0) # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- spectra & quadrature def test_spectrum_is_continuous_to_zero_and_consistent_with_differential():     assert LAW.integral(150.0) == 0.0 and LAW.integral(1e3) == 0.0     assert LAW.integral(149.999) == pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-8)     x, h = 3.0, 1e-5     num = -(LAW.integral(x + h) - LAW.integral(x - h)) / (2 * h)     assert LAW.differential(x) == pytest.approx(float(num), rel=1e-6) def test_total_flux_mass_is_exact():     one = lambda x: np.ones_like(x)     assert integral_rate(one, LAW, 1e-2) == pytest.approx(float(LAW.integral(1e-2)), rel=1e-12) def test_step_cross_section_is_exact_with_threshold_node():     sig = 3e-14     for xth in (0.5, 2.0, 17.3, 80.0):         step = lambda x, xth=xth: np.where(x > xth, sig, 0.0)         got = integral_rate(step, LAW, 1e-2, extra_nodes=[xth])         assert got == pytest.approx(sig * float(LAW.integral(xth)), rel=1e-12) def test_weibull_integral_matches_adaptive_quadrature():     cs = CrossSection(1e-14, 0.5, 20.0, 1.5)     ref, _ = quad(lambda x: float(cs(x)) * float(LAW.differential(x)), 1e-2, 150.0,                   points=[0.5, 1, 5, 20, 50], limit=2000, epsrel=1e-10)     got = integral_rate(cs, LAW, 1e-2, extra_nodes=[0.5])     assert got == pytest.approx(ref, rel=2e-3) def test_quadrature_converges_with_grid():     cs = CrossSection(1e-14, 0.5, 20.0, 1.5)     coarse = integral_rate(cs, LAW, 1e-2, n=500, extra_nodes=[0.5])     fine = integral_rate(cs, LAW, 1e-2, n=32000, extra_nodes=[0.5])     mid = integral_rate(cs, LAW, 1e-2, n=4000, extra_nodes=[0.5])     assert abs(mid - fine) < abs(coarse - fine)     assert mid == pytest.approx(fine, rel=1e-3) def test_weibull_cross_section_properties():     cs = CrossSection(2e-14, 3.0, 10.0, 2.0)     assert cs(3.0) == 0.0 and cs(0.1) == 0.0     x = np.linspace(0, 500, 1001)     s = cs(x)     assert np.all(np.diff(s) >= -1e-30) and s[-1] == pytest.approx(2e-14, rel=1e-6)     assert cs(3.0 + 10.0) == pytest.approx(2e-14 no belts, SEP events dominate def test_config_rejects_cross_section_below_integration_grid():     c = SimConfig(); c.radiation.sram.ion.x_th = 1e-3     with pytest.raises(ValueError): 1, 0)), (3, (0, 0, 1)), (4, (0, 1, 0))]:         m = EccMemory(64, 1)         m.inject(np.arange(flips))               # all in word 0 (interleave 1)         r = m.scrub(make_rng(0, "x")) def test_no_ecc_every_corrupted_word_is_silent():     m = EccMemory(64, 1, ecc=False)     m.inject([0, 100, 200]); r = m.scrub()     assert (r.corrected_words, r.due_words, r.sdc_words) == (0, 0, 3) def test_interleaving_defeats_adjacent_cluster():     for I, expect in [(4, (4, 0, 0)), (1, (0, 1, 0))]:        # 4-bit cluster         m = EccMemory(64, I); m.inject(np.arange(100, 104))         r = m.scrub(make_rng(0, "i")); assert (r.corrected_words, r.due_words, r.sdc_words) == expect     m = EccMemory(64, 2); m.inject(np.arange(0, 3))           # bits 0,1,2 -> words 0,1,0     r = m.scrub(); assert (r.corrected_words, r.due_words) == (1, 1) @pytest.mark.slow def test_double_error_probability_matches_poisson_theory():     """Words with exactly 2 hits per scrub interval: n_words * mu^2/2 * exp(-mu) * (63/64)."""     rng = make_rng(11, "acc")     n_words, mu = 2_000_000, 0.05     m = EccMemory(64, 1)     k = int(rng.poisson(mu * n_words))     m.inject(rng.integers(0, n_words * 64, size=k))     r = m.scrub(make_rng(0, "s"))     due_exp = n_words * mu ** 2 / 2 * math.exp(-mu) * 63 / 64     corr_exp = n_words * mu * math.exp(-mu) * (1 - mu / 64)          # distinct-bit single hits     assert r.due_words == pytest.approx(due_exp, rel=0.08)     assert r.corrected_words == pytest.approx(corr_exp, rel=0.02) @pytest.mark.slow def test_due_rate_proportional_to_scrub_interval():     """Fixed exposure: DUE ~ lambda^2 * tau  => doubling the scrub interval doubles DUE."""     n_words, lam_w, total = 1_000_000, 0.02, 20.0        # hits / word / s, seconds     def due(tau):         rng = make_rng(5, "tau", tau)         m, d = EccMemory(64, 1), 0         for _ in range(int(total / tau)):             k = int(rng.poisson(lam_w * tau * n_words))             m.inject(rng.integers(0, n_words * 64, size=k))             d += m.scrub(make_rng(0, "s")).due_words         return d     d1, d2 = due(1.0), due(2.0)     assert 1.8 < d2 / d1 < 2.15 # --------------------------------------------------------------------------- subsystem with a stub environment class Stub:     def __init__(self, **lam): self.lam = lam     def expected(self, region, n, t0, t1): return self.lam.get(region, 0.0) * (t1 - t0) def _sub(cfg=None, seed=0, **lam):     cfg = (cfg or SimConfig()).validate()     return RadiationSubsystem(cfg, Stub(**lam), make_rng(seed, "rad")) def test_mbu_fraction_and_cluster_size_distribution():     s = _sub(sram=2e5)     s.expose(0.0, 1.0, 1e9, 1.5)     L = s.ledger     assert L.n_seu_sram == pytest.approx(2e5, rel=0.02)     assert L.n_mbu / L.n_seu_sram == pytest.approx(0.10, abs=0.005)     sizes = _sub()._cluster_sizes(200_000)     assert sizes.min() == 2 and sizes.max() == 6     assert sizes.mean() == pytest.approx(2.9375, abs=0.02) def test_ecc_with_interleave_absorbs_all_mbu_but_without_it_fails():     c = SimConfig(); c.radiation.mbu_prob = 1.0; c.radiation.mbu_max_cluster = 4; and s.ledger.sdc_sram == 0     assert s.ledger.words_corrected > s.ledger.n_seu_sram         # clusters corrected word by word     c = SimConfig(); c.radiation.mbu_prob = 1.0; build_transformer_workload, profile_pass, run_ensemble,                   run_mission, run_workload) from uchu.config import OrbitalConfig, OrbitType, SolarActivity from uchu.orbit import FlareSchedule LAYERS = build_transformer_workload(num_layers=2) def hot_cfg(factor=1e8, **over):     """Cross-sections inflated so rare events become countable in short, fast tests."""     c = SimConfig()     for reg in (c.radiation.sram, c.radiation.register, c.radiation.logic_set):         reg.ion.sigma_sat_cm2 *= factor         reg.proton.sigma_sat_cm2 *= factor     c.radiation.scrub_interval_s = 1.0     for k, v in over.items():         setattr(c, k, v)     return c.validate() # ---------------------------------------------------------------- single clock & determinism def test_run_does_not_touch_global_rngs():     np.random.seed(1); random.seed(1)     n0, p0 = np.random.get_state()[1].copy(), random.getstate()     run_workload(hot_cfg(), LAYERS, n_passes=20)     run_mission(hot_cfg(), LAYERS, 300.0)     assert np.array_equal(n0, np.random.get_state()[1]) and p0 == random.getstate() def test_mission_clock_is_exact_and_energy_is_consistent():     r = run_mission(SimConfig(), LAYERS, 12345.6)     assert r.simulated_s == pytest.approx(12345.6, abs=1e-6) and r.completed     assert r.energy_j == pytest.approx(r.avg_power_w * r.simulated_s) np.ones(len(draws)) / len(draws)", [CW]),     ("step failures abort silently as ok", "uchu/calibration/workflow.py", "            steps.append(StepResult(name, \"failed\", error=str(e)))", "            steps.append(StepResult(name, \"ok\", {}))", [CW]),     ("input config mutated in place", "uchu/calibration/workflow.py", "    cfg = cfg.copy().validate()\n    steps", "    cfg = cfg.validate()\n    steps", [CW]), ] def run_one(name, rel, old, new, tests):     with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:         dst = pathlib.Path(td) / "w"         shutil.copytree(ROOT, dst, ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns("__pycache__", ".pytest_cache", ".coverage", "tools", "*.pyc"))         f = dst / rel         src = f.read_text()         if old not in src:             return "SETUP-ERROR", f"pattern not found in {rel}"         f.write_text(src.replace(old, new, 1))         r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "-q", "-x", "-p", "no:cacheprovider", "-m", "not slow", *tests],                            cwd=dst, capture_output=True, text=True)         first = next((l for l in r.stdout.splitlines() if l.startswith("FAILED") or l.startswith("ERROR")), "")         return ("KILLED" if r.returncode != 0 else "SURVIVED"), first def main() -> int:     flt = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else ""     bad = []     sel = [m for m in MUTANTS if flt in m[0]]     for name, rel, old, new, tests in sel:         status, first = run_one(name, rel, old, new, tests)         print(f"[{status:8s}] {name}   {first.split(' - ')[0][-70:]}", flush=True)         if status != "KILLED":             bad.append(name)     print(f"\n{len(sel) - len(bad)}/{len(sel)} mutants killed")     return 1 if bad else 0 if __name__ == "__main__":     sys.exit(main()) ``` ### `tools/demo.py` ```python """Demo: 3 LEO orbits of a 12-layer INT8 transformer; prints a compact, reproducible summary.""" import sys, pathlib sys.path.insert(0, str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent)) from uchu import SimConfig, build_transformer_workload, run_mission, run_workload cfg = SimConfig(random_seed=42) cfg.thermal.capacitance_j_per_k = 200.0 layers = build_transformer_workload(num_layers=12) w = run_workload(cfg, layers, n_passes=100) print(f"[workload] {w.n_passes} passes, {w.elapsed_s*1e3:.2f} ms simulated") print(f"  dense-equivalent throughput : {w.avg_tflops_dense:7.2f} TFLOPS   power {w.avg_power_w:6.2f} W   "       f"({w.tflops_per_watt:.2f} TFLOPS/W)   memory-bound time {w.memory_bound_fraction:.1%}") m = run_mission(cfg, layers, mission_s=3 * 5739.0, duty=0.8, dt_max_s=10.0) r = m.radiation print(f"[mission ] {m.simulated_s/3600:.2f} h simulated, completed={m.completed}, failed={m.failed}") print(f"  temperature  min/mean/max   : {m.T_min_k:6.1f} / {m.T_mean_k:6.1f} / {m.T_max_k:6.1f} K   throttled {m.throttled_fraction:.1%}") print(f"  power / throughput           : {m.avg_power_w:6.2f} W / {m.avg_tflops_dense:6.2f} TFLOPS (duty 0.8)") print(f"  SRAM SEUs / MBU / corrected  : {r.n_seu_sram} / {r.n_mbu} / {r.words_corrected}   DUE {r.words_due}   SDC {r.sdc_total}") lo, hi = r.sdc_per_hr_ci() print(f"  SDC rate 95% CI (per hour)   : [{lo:.3g}, {hi:.3g}]   availability {r.availability:.4f}") print(f"  provenance                   : {m.provenance['model_version']} cfg={m.provenance['config_hash']} seed={m.provenance['seed']}") ``` ### `tools/calibration_demo.py` ```python """End-to-end calibration demo on a SYNTHETIC campaign (hidden truth -> CSV files -> plan.json -> CLI -> report).     python tools/calibration_demo.py [work_dir] Shows the exact file formats and the exact command a real campaign would use.  Synthetic data prove the machinery recovers known parameters; they do not prove the models describe real hardware. """ import json, pathlib, sys, tempfile sys.path.insert(0, str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent)) from uchu import SimConfig, make_rng from uchu.calibration.registry import get_by_path from uchu.calibration.synthetic import analyst_start_config, make_truth_config, write_campaign from uchu.calibration.workflow import _static_rate_per_bit_day, main work = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="uchu_cal_")) truth = make_truth_config() plan = write_campaign(work / "campaign", truth, analyst_start_config(truth), make_rng(2024, "demo")) print(f"campaign files: {sorted(p.name for p in plan.parent.iterdir())}\n") code = main([str(plan), "--out", str(work / "out")]) cfg = SimConfig.from_json((work / "out" / "calibrated_config.json").read_text()) rows = [(p, get_by_path(truth, p), e) for p, e in sorted(cfg.calibration.items()) if e["ci95"] and p != "radiation.avf_sram"] inside = sum(e["ci95"][0] <= tv <= e["ci95"][1] for _, tv, e in rows) print(f"\nhidden truth inside the 95 % interval: {inside}/{len(rows)} parameters") tr = _static_rate_per_bit_day(truth, "sram") rs = json.loads((work / "out" / "calibration_report.json").read_text())["rate_summary"]["sram"] print(f"SRAM upset rate / bit / day: truth {tr:.3e} | shipped placeholder {rs['placeholder']:.3e} | "       f"calibrated {rs['median']:.3e} [{rs['lo']:.3e}, {rs['hi']:.3e}]") print(f"outputs in {work / 'out'}") sys.exit(code) ``` ### `pyproject.toml` ```toml [project] name = "uchu" version = "3.0.0" requires-python = ">=3.10" dependencies = ["numpy>=1.24", "scipy>=1.11"] [project.optional-dependencies] test = ["pytest>=7.4"] [tool.pytest.ini_options] testpaths = ["tests"] addopts = "-q" markers = ["slow: statistical / long-running tests"] ``` ### `requirements.txt` ```text numpy>=1.24 scipy>=1.11 pytest>=7.4 ```
